# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-153 reconciliation outcome correction

- Corrected recovery semantics so `SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE` is recorded only when a
  recovery/backfill actually changes candle evidence; a competing live or
  existing row is not relabelled.
- `REST_NOT_FINAL` is now retryable rather than a terminal mismatch. Audit keys
  use fixed epoch-millisecond identity precision, and reconciliation is safely
  disabled by default even when configuration is absent.
- Added terminal outcomes for worker data-unavailable, historical-only,
  already-analysed, stale work, and detector expiry paths. Transient failures
  remain without a terminal outcome so retry remains possible.
- Added `LocalReconciliationAuditRepositoryTest`; targeted Java 21 tests and
  compile passed. MySQL, race/crash, rate-limit, barrier, effect-reconciler,
  and Replay validation remain open.

# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-153 reconciliation safety correction

- Recovery now requires positive loss evidence before it may finalise an open
  row: either the successor candle is already present or a reconnect was
  admitted after the candidate close. This prevents a short healthy-stream
  delay from being converted into a token-free, unanalysed close.
- REST finality/identity disagreement is retryable while the local row remains
  open. The authoritative Binance close timestamp, not the local expected
  value, is persisted after a successful exact match. A bounded first-candle
  probe identifies pre-listing identities as explicit unavailable outcomes.
- Recovery uses the short timeout client and checks drain between requests;
  late-live-close evidence now preserves its first timestamp.
- Status: Java 21 compilation passed. Dedicated reconciliation race, drain,
  MySQL, and Replay tests remain required before enabling the feature.

# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-153 deterministic reconciliation test coverage

- Added deterministic Java 21 tests for the reconciliation contract: a healthy
  late live close without successor/reconnect evidence is left untouched;
  successor evidence permits update-only repair and records a non-live outcome;
  a gap-insert race ends as `ALREADY_PRESENT` without inventing an outcome;
  a confirmed exchange gap ends as `SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE`; and active drain
  prevents REST admission.
- Corrected the audit test to use a true terminal state (`EXPIRED`) rather than
  `REST_MISMATCH`, which is deliberately retryable.
- Extended the disposable MySQL integration test with actual affected-row
  checks for update-only authoritative repair and insert-only gap backfill.

  environment variables. Reconciliation remains disabled by default.

===== codex-docs/TRADE_INVESTIGATION_AGENT.md =====
# Trade Investigation Agent

## Role

Investigate one Crypto AI Trader trade from durable evidence. Explain what
happened, why the system entered and exited, whether it followed the intended
rules, and whether the entry/exit was supported by the information available
at that time.

Investigations are read-only. Do not change code, configuration, services,
database records, review states, Replay work, or deployment state.

## Required input

Obtain or resolve:

- Environment and application: local, test, or production.
- Database host, port, and schema.
- One trade reference: `paper_position.id`, `wallet_trade.id`, or a symbol plus
  a bounded approximate entry-time window.
- Optional question or suspected issue.

Before analysis, confirm the symbol, trade/position ID, running version or FIX
level, and ingestion mode. Read the actual schema before choosing tables or
columns. Do not assume a table is in `crypto_ai` when it may belong to
`crypto_ai_v2`.

## Evidence rules

- Use configured database and SSH/log connections without displaying secrets.
- Use bounded, read-only queries and targeted log searches.
- Treat stored timestamps as UTC; display both UTC and KSA (`UTC+03:00`).
- Attach record IDs and timestamped log evidence to every factual conclusion.
- Distinguish **confirmed fact**, **supported inference**, and **missing or
  conflicting evidence**.
- Missing logs or records do not prove that no execution/effect occurred.
- A saved signal does not prove execution. A callback return does not prove a
  transaction committed. Under FIX-153, `market_price_event.APPLIED` proves a
  committed successful protection callback, but can be a no-op when there was
  no open position; find the position/trade mutation separately.
- Current configuration does not prove which settings were active at entry. If
  no configuration history exists, report that limitation.

## Investigation workflow

### 1. Resolve identity and environment

Link the trade reference to, where available:

- `paper_position`
- `wallet_trade`
- entry and exit `trade_signal`
- `signal_processing_work`
- wallet/position execution records

Record entry and exit times in UTC and KSA. Confirm whether the trader ran in
local-candle OFF mode or a legacy shared-market mode.

### 2. Collect a small evidence bundle

Start with exact IDs and a narrow time window. Expand only when the evidence
requires it. Collect:

| Evidence | Establishes |
| --- | --- |
| `paper_position` | lifecycle, entry/exit, stop, target, status |
| `wallet_trade` | executed quantity, prices, fees, linked signal |
| `trade_signal` | decision, strategy, scores, entry eligibility, reasons |
| `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempt, completion, expiry, uncertainty |
| `candle` / `technical_indicator` | market data and analysis inputs available then |
| `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and outcomes |
| configuration/version evidence | applicable rules, when historically available |
| application and timing logs | analysis, execution, protection, exceptions, waits |

Avoid fetching large signal explanation fields until they are needed.

### 3. Reconstruct the timeline

Build a timeline, explicitly showing missing stages:

`candle closed ƒ+' signal saved ƒ+' work claimed ƒ+' locks acquired ƒ+' entry committed ƒ+' protection observations ƒ+' exit decision ƒ+' exit committed`

Measure separately:

- candle close to signal generation;
- signal generation to processing/entry;
- observation time to protection completion;
- exit-trigger evidence to recorded exit.

Correlate delays only with logs/events for the same symbol, position, signal,
or price-event identity. A nearby slow SQL query is not proof of causation.

### 4. Assess entry correctness and quality

Use only evidence that was available at entry. Check:

- signal mode, closed-candle identity, freshness, and data quality;
- final decision and entry-allowed result;
- BTC context, higher-timeframe, ATR, liquidity, risk/reward, sizing, and
  strategy gates that applied at that time;
- correct signal/position linkage and absence of duplicate/stale-retry entry;
- quantity, wallet balance, risk limits, fees, and fill/reference-price gap.

Return two separate conclusions:

- **Execution correctness:** followed or violated the implemented rules.
- **Entry quality:** supported, weak, or unverifiable from contemporaneous
  evidence.

Subsequent price movement may evaluate the outcome; it must never be presented
as information the entry decision had.

### 5. Reconstruct protection and stop-loss behavior

For FIX-153, inspect accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events and retain
`PENDING`, `NON_CANONICAL`, and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` records as evidence
gaps or exclusions. Never substitute candle OHLC for absent intra-candle price
evidence.

For each relevant accepted observation:

1. Determine the then-active stop, target, trailing stop, and profit-lock state.
2. Find the first accepted observation that met an exit threshold.
3. Confirm that protection evaluated it and identify the resulting position or
   wallet execution record.
4. Explain delays, rejections, stale/out-of-order classification, exceptions,
   lock waits, or pool pressure.

Classify the exit path accurately: fixed stop-loss, trailing stop, profit-lock,
take-profit, signal-driven exit, manual exit, or unknown.

### 6. Assess exit correctness and quality

Compare the recorded exit reason, quantity, and price with trigger evidence.
Check threshold reach, accepted-observation identity, protection timing, and
the committed execution. Calculate maximum favourable/adverse movement during
the holding period separately from the contemporaneous exit assessment.

### 7. Reconcile financial results

Recalculate using execution records:

- bought/sold quantities and weighted entry/exit prices;
- gross P&L, fees, and net P&L;
- partial exits, remaining quantity, and realized versus unrealized values.

Flag discrepancies among wallet records, position records, and UI output.

### 8. Diagnose cause and use Replay carefully

Classify findings as one or more of:

- strategy decision;
- data freshness or coverage;
- analysis delay;
- execution failure;
- protection failure;
- database, lock, or connection-pool contention;
- accounting/UI discrepancy;
- insufficient evidence.

Run Replay only as a controlled comparison with equivalent candles, accepted
prices, strategy version, and settings. State every unavailable input or
material difference. Never claim parity by substituting candle OHLC for missing
price evidence or treating `PENDING` as accepted.

## Required final report

1. **Trade identity and environment** ƒ?" IDs, symbol, version/mode, scope.
2. **UTC/KSA timeline** ƒ?" evidence-backed sequence and latency breakdown.
3. **Entry** ƒ?" execution correctness and entry quality.
4. **Exit** ƒ?" execution correctness and exit quality.
5. **Stop-loss/profit-lock** ƒ?" worked, failed, or unverifiable, with evidence.
6. **P&L reconciliation** ƒ?" quantities, prices, fees, and discrepancies.
7. **Root cause and confidence** ƒ?" confirmed defect, valid loss, configuration
   issue, delay, or insufficient evidence.
8. **Evidence references** ƒ?" record IDs, query/log timestamps, code/config
   references.
9. **Missing evidence** ƒ?" exactly what prevents a stronger conclusion.
10. **Recommended correction and regression checks** ƒ?" only when justified.

A profitable trade can violate the rules, and a losing trade can be a correct
execution. State those independently.


===== claude-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md =====
# Crypto AI Trader ƒ?" Architecture (Claude)

Maintained by Claude. Based on a read-only review of the working tree on
2026-10-09 (branch `master`, including uncommitted FIX-153 changes). Line
numbers are approximate. `codex-docs/` is maintained separately by Codex.

Status labels: **Current** (in the working tree), **Pending** (design only),
**Legacy/shared** (retained shared-market path).

## Release status

- FIX-153 (trader-owned local candles, `shared-market.mode=OFF`): implemented
  in source, **not approved for production**. Java 21 build/tests, disposable
  MySQL bootstrap, crash/race, Windows two-process, soak and Replay-parity
  gates are open. This workstation has Java 17 and cannot compile the project.
- FIX-153 work is **uncommitted**: ~67 modified tracked files plus untracked
  `com/crypto/local/`, `com/crypto/infrastructure/diagnostics/`, migrations
  `V99`ƒ?"`V106` (except the Java `V103`) and `B105`.
- FIX-154 (higher-timeframe BUY validation): pending, not implemented.

## Stack

Spring Boot 4.1.0, Java 21, MySQL 8 (`docker-compose.yml`: `mysql:8.4`,
host port 3307), Flyway (`V1`ƒ?"`V106`, plus baseline `B105`), Spring Security
form login, Jenkins + `scripts/deploy/Deploy-Trader.ps1`. Paper trading only;
no live-exchange orders.

## Databases

| Schema | Owner | Trader use |
|---|---|---|
| `crypto_ai` | Trader | Only runtime DB in OFF mode. |
| `crypto_ai_v2` | Collector | Read in LIVE/OBSERVE via `SharedMarketSource` read-only pool. Must not be touched in OFF mode. |

`V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` (Java migration) adds indexes to
`crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`; a fresh trader-only install fails
without that schema. Release blocker; do not edit applied migrations.

## Ingestion modes (`shared-market.mode`, default `OFF`)

- **OFF (FIX-153)** ƒ?" trader connects to Binance and writes `crypto_ai.candle`.
  `LocalTraderInstance` requires an absolute `local-candle.instance-lock-path`
  (env `LOCAL_TRADER_INSTANCE_LOCK`) and holds an OS file lock: one trader
  process per host. Empty value fails OFF-mode startup.
- **LIVE / OBSERVE (legacy/shared)** ƒ?" `SharedMarketConsumer` polls collector
  stream tables into local `shared_market_event_delivery`;
  `SharedAnalysisDispatcher` drives analysis. Direct ingestion entry points
  (`BinanceWebSocketManager`, `BinanceKlineService.processKline`,
  `MarketDataService.importCandles`, `MarketDataBootstrapService`) are
  disabled in LIVE. The two paths must never run together.

## Primary flow (OFF mode)

```text
Binance WS (combined stream, enabled coins x 1m/5m/1h)
 -> BinanceWebSocketManager (owner thread "fix131-ws-owner", generation gate)
 -> BinanceWebSocketHandler.handleTextMessage (socket thread, synchronous)
 -> BinanceKlineService.processKline        (@Transactional NEVER, drain check)
 -> KlineTransactionCoordinator.process     (each phase REQUIRES_NEW, READ_COMMITTED, 30s)
     1 INPUT        CandleRepository.upsertRuntime + MarketPriceEventService.recordPending (1m)
     2 PROTECTION   LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice (1m; one retry on initial-lock deadlock)
     3 OBSERVER     PriceMoveMonitorService.onPrice (errors swallowed)
     4 CLOSE        publish CandleClosedEvent only if this receipt won the 0->1 close token
 -> CandleClosedEventListener (AFTER_COMMIT, not @Async)
 -> CandleAnalysisDispatcher (per symbol|interval lane)
 -> candleAnalysisExecutor (core 8 / max 16 / queue 200, AbortPolicy)
 -> CandleClosedAnalysisWorker.processShared
 -> TechnicalIndicatorService -> AnalysisService -> TradeSignal + signal_processing_work
 -> SignalProcessingCoordinator -> PaperTradingService -> WalletAutoExecutionService
```

All candle writes and live position protection run on the single WebSocket
socket thread, one message at a time. Analysis never runs on it.

## Candle persistence

- `CandleRepository.upsertRuntime/write`: `INSERT ... ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE`
  on `uk_candle_symbol_interval_open_time`. Once `closed=1` every column keeps
  its old value (monotonic). RUNTIME writes set `close_transition_token` only
  on the 0->1 transition; the read-back token equal to this receipt's UUID is
  the sole authority to dispatch a close. BACKFILL writes never set a token.
- `domain/Candle` is now a read DTO.

## Recovery and gaps

- `LocalClosedCandleDetector` (every 2s, OFF only): re-admits tokened, non-
  BACKFILL closed candles with no `trade_signal` and no
  `signal_processing_work` (up to 6 retries). Repairs commit-to-dispatch gaps,
  **not** missing close facts.
- `CandleGapDiagnostics` (every 10s, 72h horizon): logs `DB_CANDLE_GAP` only,
  `repair=false`.
- REST backfill (`MarketDataService.importCandles`, BACKFILL provenance,
  skips still-open candle, server time fetched before klines, no HTTP inside
  the transaction) runs only at startup (`MarketDataBootstrapService`) or coin
  activation.
- **Gap:** a WebSocket reconnect at a candle-close boundary can leave rows at
  `closed=0` (Binance does not replay the final closed kline). Nothing repairs
  this automatically; with `maximum-missing-candles: 0` it blocks analysis for
  that symbol/interval. Deployment gate.

## WebSocket lifecycle

`BinanceWebSocketManager` ticks every 500 ms on its owner thread; connect and
close run on separate workers. Each attempt gets a new
`BinanceWebSocketHandler` + `GenerationGate` (PENDING -> ADMITTING -> REVOKED);
messages outside ADMITTING / the primary session are dropped. Handshake
deadline 15s. Failure backoff = `websocket.health-check-seconds` (15s);
`websocket.reconnect-delay-seconds` is only displayed by
`BinanceStatusController` and does not drive reconnects. 30s silence only logs
`NO_MESSAGES`. Coin changes call `reload()` (bounded mailbox, 30s wait).
Symbols come from `CoinConfigurationService.enabledSymbols()` (DB), not yml.

## Analysis

- `CandleAnalysisDispatcher`: one active task per lane. 1m keeps only the
  newest pending candle (`PENDING_REPLACED`); higher intervals FIFO capped at
  4 (`LANE_OVERFLOW`). Coordinates live and detector admissions.
- `CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator`: 1024 striped locks by
  symbol+interval+openTime; detector uses `tryLock`.
- `CandleClosedAnalysisWorker` stages: retry resume -> coordination lock ->
  `CandleDataQualityService` (>=210 candles, 0 gaps, valid OHLCV) ->
  indicators -> existing-signal check -> freshness (`SharedEventPolicy`;
  1m 90s, 5m 120s, 1h 300s, 4h 600s, 1d 1800s after close) -> LIVE analysis +
  registration, or `HISTORICAL_ONLY` analysis for stale input -> signal
  processing.
- Signal decision details: see `TRADING_RULES.md`.

## Execution

- `SignalProcessingStore`: `signal_processing_work` row created in the same
  transaction as the `trade_signal`. Claim (REQUIRES_NEW, 3s, FOR UPDATE)
  sets RUNNING + owner token. States: PENDING, RUNNING, COMPLETED, EXPIRED,
  RETRYABLE_FAILURE, SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY (1/3/10s backoff), LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED,
  REVIEW_REQUIRED (never auto-replayed). RUNNING > 5 min is quarantined.
- `SignalProcessingCoordinator.process` business transaction: symbol lock
  (`wallet_symbol_coordination`) -> ownership recheck -> position lock ->
  lineage recheck -> `SignalProcessingFreshness` (after locks, DB clock with
  exchange correction) -> `PaperTradingService.processSignalBody` ->
  `store.complete`.
- `SignalProcessingRecovery` (every 30s) resumes due WORKER/RECOVERY rows.
- `WalletAutoExecutionService`: lock order symbol -> position ->
  `wallet_mutation_coordination` -> daily stats. Idempotent by unique
  `wallet_trade.execution_key`. `WalletMutationRepositoryGuard` rejects wallet
  writes without the mutation lock.

## Price evidence (`market_price_event`)

1m direct observations: `recordPending` (INPUT tx; rejects >2s future, >15s
stale vs exchange-corrected JVM clock; duplicate timestamp -> NON_CANONICAL)
-> `finish` in PROTECTION tx -> APPLIED or NON_CANONICAL.
`resolvePending` (every 5s, OFF) marks PENDING > 120s and not in flight as
PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED (never replayed). Replay and execution price authority
use only LEGACY/APPLIED rows. `ExchangeClockSynchronizer` measures Binance
time vs DB and JVM (dedicated 5s client, no HTTP under locks).

## Other components

| Area | Key classes | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Order book | `OrderBookLiquidityService`, `OrderBookSnapshotService` | REST depth every 5s; best-effort async persistence, drops on overflow. |
| Replay | `RegressionTestWorker`, `ShadowProductionReplayService`, `ReplayParityValidator` | Same production policy beans; simulated wallet; exact parity only with stored price events (`EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY`), else `SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK`. Writes only `*_test` / archive tables. |
| Inspector / activity | `TradeInspectorService`, `TradeActivityService` | Read-only except review marks / copy-to-proven. |
| Health | `SystemHealthDailyService`, local-candle health | OFF-mode must not query retired shared tables. |
| Monitor | `PriceMoveMonitorService`, `PriceMoveFinalizationWorker` | "Catching Market" retrospective blame. |
| Audit | `ProductionExitAuditService` | Records real close trigger. |
| Drain | `LocalDrainService` | ADMIN-only; stops admission, exits JVM on quiesce, resumes on timeout. |
| Security | `SecurityConfig` | Form login, `app_user`; `/api/local/drain`, `/api/fix142/**` ADMIN. |
| Whale | `whale/*` | Optional schedulers when `whale.enabled`. |
| Approval console | `com.fix132.approval` | Separate app outside `com.crypto` scan; shared-market cutover only. |

## Deployment

Jenkins: JDK 21 `mvn clean package` -> `Deploy-Trader.ps1` (mutex, single
FIX-153 jar, SHA256, refuses unknown port-8080 owner, requires graceful
`-DrainCommand`, never force-kills, verifies child PID and OFF guard). No
automatic rollback. No deployment or production change without explicit user
approval.


===== claude-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md =====
# Investigation Log ƒ?" Claude

Findings and rejected approaches recorded by Claude. Earlier findings by Codex
(2026-10-09 candle coverage, signal staleness after reconnect, local repair of
42 rows, ETHUSDT paper trade 59) are in `codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md`.

## 2026-10-09 ƒ?" Read-only codebase review: observations

Static code review only; no database queries, logs or Replay runs.

1. **Reconnect close-boundary gap is not auto-repaired.**
   `CandleGapDiagnostics` logs `DB_CANDLE_GAP` with `repair=false`;
   `LocalClosedCandleDetector` only re-admits candles that already have a
   runtime close token. A row left at `closed=0` by a reconnect stays open, and
   `maximum-missing-candles: 0` then blocks analysis for that lane. Matches the
   Codex-recorded outage of 2026-10-09 and the late ETH entry (trade 59).
2. **Automatic trades record zero fees.** `WalletAutoExecutionService` writes
   `feeUsdt(ZERO)` for buy and all exit paths (lines ~173, 260, 349, 443, 540,
   653). Automated P&L is gross; investigations must compute fees separately.
3. **Two independent sizing models.** Paper position is risk-sized from
   `paperAccountBalance`; wallet execution is budget-sized. The paper row
   adopts the wallet quantity. Paper and managed position state are synced by
   specific fixes (FIX-067 TP, partial-harvest sync), not one model.
4. **Account risk limits unused in automatic entry.**
   `CryptoAccountConfigurationService` limits (max order, exposure, loss,
   slippage) are not read by the paper entry path.
5. **Single-threaded ingestion and protection.** Candle upserts, price events
   and `LivePositionProtectionService` run synchronously on the WebSocket
   socket thread; slow protection delays all later kline messages.
6. **`websocket.reconnect-delay-seconds` is display-only.** Reconnect backoff
   uses `health-check-seconds`; the property is only returned by
   `BinanceStatusController`.
7. **Wallet exit failure does not block paper close.** In
   `PaperTradingService.completeClose` a wallet exit exception is logged and
   the paper position still closes ƒ?" a potential paper/wallet divergence to
   check in investigations.
8. **Fresh trader-only install blocked by V103** (collector schema
   dependency); known release blocker.
9. **FIX-153 work is uncommitted** in the working tree (including V99-V106,
   B105 and `com/crypto/local`).

Items 2-7 are observations, not confirmed defects; whether each is intended
has not been confirmed with the user.


===== claude-docs/TRADING_RULES.md =====
# Crypto AI Trader ƒ?" Trading Rules (Claude)

Maintained by Claude. Describes trading behavior as implemented in the
working tree on 2026-10-09 (read-only review). Values are defaults from code
or `application.yml`; verify current configuration before relying on them.
Paper trading only.

## 1. Signal admission

- Only closed candles are analysed (1m, 5m, 1h for enabled coins).
- Data quality (`CandleDataQualityService`): at least 210 closed candles,
  `maximum-missing-candles: 0`, valid OHLCV. Any gap -> `DATA_QUALITY_BLOCKED`.
  Do not relax this to work around ingestion gaps; restore the missing candles.
- Freshness after close (`SharedEventPolicy` / `SignalProcessingFreshness`):
  1m 90s, 5m 2 min, 1h 5 min, 4h 10 min, 1d 30 min. Stale input produces a
  `HISTORICAL_ONLY` signal that never trades.
- Every analysis persists a `trade_signal`, including neutral/no-entry ones.
- `trading.scheduled-analysis-enabled: false` (legacy recovery scheduler off).

## 2. Scoring (`AnalysisService`)

Base categories: trend (max 25), momentum (15), volume (20), sentiment (15),
fundamentals (10). Unavailable sentiment/fundamentals are removed from the
maximum. Indicators: EMA20/50/200, SMA20, RSI14, MACD, Bollinger(20,2),
ATR14, volume SMA20, RVOL(20).

Regime (`MarketRegimeService` + `RegimeStateService`): UNKNOWN, BREAKOUT /
BREAKOUT_CANDIDATE, HIGH_VOLATILITY, STRONG/WEAK UP/DOWN, RANGE.

Strategy (`MarketStrategyService`):
- NO_TRADE if data invalid or regime UNKNOWN.
- DEFENSIVE if risky liquidity, crowded futures, sentiment coverage < 50%,
  higher-timeframe STRONG_CONFLICT, BTC STRONG_CONFLICT with influence >= 0.70,
  or low regime confidence.
- Otherwise TREND_FOLLOWING, RANGE_MEAN_REVERSION or BREAKOUT; score rescaled
  to 0-100 with per-profile thresholds (`DynamicStrategyProperties`).
- ATR `NO_ENTRY` downgrades BUY to WATCH.

## 3. Final decision chain (`FinalDecisionService`)

Ordered: STRATEGY_SCORE -> DATA_QUALITY -> ATR_RISK -> RANGE_ENTRY_LOCATION ->
MULTI_TIMEFRAME -> BTC_CONTEXT -> DERIVATIVES_POSITIONING -> ORDER_BOOK. Each
stage can VETO, DOWNGRADE, PASS or be UNAVAILABLE; `entryAllowed` is ANDed.
`primaryBlockingStage` = first blocker. Confidence weights: regime 25%, data
15%, timeframe 20%, BTC 15%, liquidity 12%, derivatives 8%, sentiment 10%;
capped at 49 when entry is blocked.

Multi-timeframe context: 1m uses 5m+1h; 5m uses 15m+1h; 1h uses 4h+1d.

BTC context (`BtcMarketContextService`): not applicable to BTCUSDT. Freshness
measured from BTC candle close to evaluation time (1m 120s, 5m 600s, 1h
7200s...); any freshness failure -> `STALE_CONTEXT`, entry not allowed. Fewer
than 120 samples -> LEARNING. Opposing direction with positive correlation ->
CONFLICT; |corr| >= 0.70 -> STRONG_CONFLICT (WATCH, veto if configured).

## 4. Entry (execution time)

- Only 1m signals can open a position.
- `TradeExecutionValidationService.validateBuy`: requires fresh 5m (<= 20 min)
  and 1h (<= 3 h) context; order: BUY_CONTINUATION, MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION,
  MISSING_1H_CONTEXT, `5M_BEARISH_VETO`, `1H_BEARISH_VETO`, then profile
  (CONSERVATIVE/BALANCED/AGGRESSIVE). BALANCED allows a 25% position for 5m
  NEUTRAL + 1h bullish (confidence >= 72, entry quality >= 70, BTC not
  CONFLICT/STRONG_CONFLICT/STALE) and for 5m NEUTRAL + 1h WATCH.
- `OpportunityConsolidationService`: same vetoes, then >= 3 consecutive fresh
  1m BUYs with average score >= 75 and confidence >= 70, else `QUALITY_DECAY`.
- `ExecutionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuyInternal` routes (progressive,
  retracement, continuation, HTF transition, ATR authority, direct BUY,
  consolidation, pressure probe, recovery, accumulated evidence) each through
  entry-quality and anti-chase guards.
- `StopLossEvidenceService` (FIX-122): 1m candles closed before the last
  stop-loss are excluded as entry evidence.
- Max open positions and daily loss limit (realized P&L <= -balance x
  maxDailyLoss%) enforced.
- Fill price: latest canonical 1m `market_price_event` <= 15s old; no price ->
  no BUY. `revalidateAtExecutionPrice` rejects price <= stop or >= target and
  re-scores entry quality. `trade_signal.latestPrice` is never rewritten.

## 5. Sizing

- Paper position: risk = paperAccountBalance x riskPerTrade% x effective%;
  qty = risk / |price - SL|.
- Wallet (actual executed qty): `WalletExecutionSizingPolicy` ƒ?" spend = daily
  budget x allocation% (total capped at 100%); daily budget = (USDT - reserve)
  / maxNewPositions. Rejects ALLOCATION_FULL, DAILY_POSITION_LIMIT,
  ZERO_BUDGET, INSUFFICIENT_USDT_ABOVE_RESERVE, INVALID_PRICE, ZERO_QUANTITY.
- The paper row adopts the wallet's executed quantity.
- `CryptoAccountConfigurationService` limits are not used in the automatic
  paper entry path.

## 6. Fees

Automatic wallet trades record `feeUsdt = 0` and net = gross. Fees are applied
only to manual wallet trades. Automated P&L is therefore gross of fees.

## 7. Live protection (every accepted 1m price, `LivePositionProtectionService`)

Priority:
1. Price >= TP: extend TP by +50% of entry->TP distance
   (`PositionContinuationPolicy`, TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED) or exit TAKE_PROFIT.
2. Price <= SL: STOP_LOSS (paper status STOPPED).
3. TP-extension rebase hard floor (entry x 1.0005): PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT.
4. Profit lock triggered: PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD / HTF_EXIT / BEARISH_CONFIRMATION /
   EXIT.
5. Normal exit: HTF_SELL_CONFIRMED / SELL_CONFIRMED.
6. Near-TP failure: partial harvest of 50% once (Binance minimum enforced),
   position stays open.

Profit lock (`ProfitLockPolicy`): activates at 70% progress to TP, initial
lock 40%, 10% steps, never below entry x 1.0005, only ratchets up.

Replay mechanical ordering: TP -> SL -> Profit Lock -> normal HTF exit.

## 8. Signal-driven handling of an open position

`PositionManagementService.analyze` is advisory except STOP_LOSS. A signal
price is usable only if the candle closed at/after the position open
(`PositionPriceAuthorityPolicy`). Order: TAKE_PROFIT -> profit lock ->
STOP_LOSS -> PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT -> SELL/STRONG_SELL (suppressed while
profit lock active; must pass `validateSell`: bearish 5m, no `1H_BULLISH_VETO`).
Progressive add on 1m is possible; SL/TP only move up. Signal-path closes use
`signal.latestPrice`; live closes use the tick price.

## Pending (not implemented): FIX-154

Historical bearish 1h may block a current 1m BUY only when fresh, quality-valid
and strong (`NOT_TRUSTED` / `SOFT_CONFLICT` / `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE`); hard
veto needs fresh quality-valid bearish 5m. Legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` unchanged.

===== claude-docs/TRADE_INVESTIGATION_AGENT.md =====
# Trade Investigation Procedure (Claude)

How Claude investigates one Crypto AI Trader trade. Follows the trade
investigation rules in `AGENTS.md`. Investigations are read-only: no change to
code, configuration, services, database records, Replay state or deployment
unless the user requests a fix.

## 1. Confirm scope

- Application, environment (local / test / production), DB host/schema.
- Symbol and one trade reference: `paper_position.id`, `wallet_trade.id`, or
  symbol + bounded entry window.
- Ingestion mode (`shared-market.mode`; OFF = FIX-153 local candles) and
  version/FIX level.
- Read the actual schema before choosing tables/columns. Never display
  secrets.

## 2. Evidence (bounded, read-only queries)

| Table | Establishes |
|---|---|
| `paper_position` | lifecycle, entry/exit, SL/TP, status, close reason |
| `wallet_trade` | executed qty/prices, `execution_key`, linked signal (fees are 0 for automatic trades) |
| `wallet_managed_position` | managed SL/TP, highest price, profit-lock / near-TP state |
| `trade_signal` | decision, strategy, scores, `entryAllowed`, `primaryBlockingStage`, `signalMode`, context JSON |
| `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempts, owner, status, expiry |
| `execution_opportunity` | entry route and opportunity state |
| `candle` / `technical_indicator` | inputs available at the time; check `closed`, provenance, gaps |
| `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and status |
| `production_exit_audit` | actual close trigger |
| `position_analysis` | advisory exit recommendations |

Load large JSON fields only when needed.

## 3. Timeline (UTC stored -> show UTC and KSA, UTC+03:00)

`candle close -> signal saved -> work claimed -> entry committed -> price
observations -> exit trigger -> exit committed`. Measure each latency and mark
missing stages explicitly.

## 4. Entry assessment (information available at entry only)

Check data quality (210 candles, zero gaps), freshness, signal mode, final
decision chain, 5m/1h validation and vetoes, BTC context, consolidation, entry
route, FIX-122 stop-loss evidence exclusion, risk/reward, sizing, fill price
vs `latestPrice` (fill from price event <= 15s old). Report execution
correctness and entry quality separately.

## 5. Protection and exit

Use only accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events; treat `PENDING`,
`NON_CANONICAL`, `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` as gaps/exclusions. Never substitute
candle OHLC for missing price evidence. Apply priority TP -> SL -> hard floor
-> profit lock -> HTF/normal exit -> near-TP harvest. Identify the first
observation that met the exit threshold and the resulting committed trade.

## 6. P&L

Recompute from `wallet_trade`: quantities, weighted prices, gross P&L,
partial harvests, and fees (computed separately since automatic fees are
recorded as 0). Flag paper vs wallet vs UI discrepancies.

## 7. Cause classification

Strategy decision, data freshness/coverage (e.g. reconnect gap), analysis
delay, execution failure, protection failure, lock/pool contention,
accounting/UI discrepancy, or insufficient evidence. Use Replay only as a
controlled comparison with stored price events and only if the user agrees
(it creates Replay state).

## Report

1. What happened (identity, UTC/KSA timeline).
2. Why the system entered and exited.
3. Whether behavior followed the intended rules.
4. Confirmed defects and missing evidence.
5. Recommended correction and regression checks, if needed.

Record significant findings in `claude-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md`.


codex

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java:1-180 =====
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   6: 
   7: /**
   8:  * Pure FIX-154 BUY-context assessment. It deliberately returns effective
   9:  * decisions separately and never mutates the JPA-managed context signals.
  10:  */
  11: @Service
  12: public class HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService {
  13:     public static final int HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 70;
  14: 
  15:     public Assessment assess(TradeSignal fiveMinute, boolean fiveFresh,
  16:                              TradeSignal oneHour, boolean oneFresh) {
  17:         if (!fiveFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  18:         if (!oneFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  19: 
  20:         SignalDecision five = decision(fiveMinute);
  21:         SignalDecision one = decision(oneHour);
  22:         if (bearish(five)) {
  23:             boolean hard = hard(oneHour);
  24:             return Assessment.reject("5M_BEARISH_VETO", hard ? Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE : band(oneHour), fiveMinute, oneHour)
  25:                     .withHardConfirmation(hard);
  26:         }
  27:         if (bearish(one) && !qualityValid(oneHour)) {
  28:             return Assessment.reject("1H_BEARISH_VETO", Band.NOT_TRUSTED, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  29:         }
  30:         if (hard(oneHour)) {
  31:             return Assessment.reject("HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO", Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  32:         }
  33:         if (soft(oneHour)) {
  34:             return Assessment.allow(Band.SOFT_CONFLICT, five, SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, 25, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  35:         }
  36:         return Assessment.allow(qualityValid(oneHour) ? Band.NO_CONFLICT : Band.NOT_TRUSTED,
  37:                 five, one, null, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  38:     }
  39: 
  40:     private Band band(TradeSignal one) {
  41:         if (!qualityValid(one)) return Band.NOT_TRUSTED;
  42:         if (hard(one)) return Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE;
  43:         if (soft(one)) return Band.SOFT_CONFLICT;
  44:         return Band.NO_CONFLICT;
  45:     }
  46:     private boolean hard(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) >= HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD; }
  47:     private boolean soft(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && (decision(s) == SignalDecision.SELL || (decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) < HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD)); }
  48:     private boolean qualityValid(TradeSignal s) { return s != null && Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.getDataQualityValid()); }
  49:     private int confidence(TradeSignal s) { return s == null ? 0 : s.getConfidenceScore(); }
  50:     private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
  51:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }
  52: 
  53:     public enum Band { NOT_APPLICABLE, NOT_TRUSTED, SOFT_CONFLICT, HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, NO_CONFLICT }
  54:     public record Assessment(boolean allowed, String code, Band band, SignalDecision effectiveFiveMinute,
  55:                              SignalDecision effectiveOneHour, Integer maxInitialPercent,
  56:                              boolean hardConfirmationPresent, TradeSignal rawFiveMinute, TradeSignal rawOneHour) {
  57:         static Assessment reject(String code, Band band, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
  58:             return new Assessment(false, code, band, decisionOf(five), decisionOf(one), null, false, five, one);
  59:         }
  60:         static Assessment allow(Band band, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one, Integer cap, TradeSignal rawFive, TradeSignal rawOne) {
  61:             return new Assessment(true, "HTF_CONTEXT_ALLOWED", band, five, one, cap, false, rawFive, rawOne);
  62:         }
  63:         Assessment withHardConfirmation(boolean value) { return new Assessment(allowed, code, band, effectiveFiveMinute, effectiveOneHour, maxInitialPercent, value, rawFiveMinute, rawOneHour); }
  64:         private static SignalDecision decisionOf(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
  65:     }
  66: }

  22: /**
  23:  * Execution-only multi-timeframe validation.
  24:  *
  25:  * Frozen responsibilities:
  26:  *  - 1m: the only normal execution timeframe
  27:  *  - 5m: tactical confirmation
  28:  *  - 1h: strategic filter/veto
  29:  *
  30:  * Execution profiles affect only entry eligibility and position percentage.
  31:  * They never modify indicators, scores, or FinalDecisionService output.
  32:  */
  33: @Slf4j
  34: @Service
  35: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  36: public class TradeExecutionValidationService {
  37: 
  38:     private static final String EXECUTION_INTERVAL = "1m";
  39:     private static final String CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL = "5m";
  40:     private static final String TREND_INTERVAL = "1h";
  41:     private static final Duration ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(10);
  42:     private static final Duration FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(20);
  43:     private static final Duration ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofHours(3);
  44: 
  45:     // FIX-11G: this mirrors the existing global CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF in
  46:     // ExecutionIntelligenceService. We deliberately reuse the proven execution-quality
  47:     // boundary instead of inventing a new confidence/quality threshold for this one route.
  48:     // The downstream global Entry Quality guard still remains authoritative and re-checks it.
  49:     private static final int BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY = 50;
  50: 
  51:     private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
  52:     private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
  53:     private final EntryConsumptionPolicy entryConsumptionPolicy;
  54:     private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
  55:     @Autowired(required = false)
  56:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  57:     @Autowired(required = false)
  58:     private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
  59:     @Autowired(required = false)
  60:     private Fix154Metrics fix154Metrics;
  61: 
  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  65: 
  66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }
  99: 
 100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
 101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 103:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
 104:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
 105:             // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
 106:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 107:                     executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
 108:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 109:         };
 110:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 111:         audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 112:         return result;
 113:     }
 114: 
 115:     /**
 116:      * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
 117:      * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
 118:      * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
 119:      * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
 120:      * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the
 121:      * normal BUY path would accept.
 122:      */
 123:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 124:         return validateBuyContext(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE");
 125:     }
 126: 
 127:     /** The caller path is audit evidence only; context eligibility remains identical. */
 128:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String auditPath) {
 129:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 130:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution reference signal is missing required timing data.");
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 134:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 135:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 136:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 137:         }
 138: 
 139:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 140:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
 141:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
 142:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 143:         }
 144: 
 145:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
 146:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
 147:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
 148:             audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 149:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 150:         }
 151: 
 152:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings().getExecutionProfile());
 153:         TradeSignal oneMinuteQuality = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
 154:         TradeSignal qualitySignal = oneMinuteQuality == null ? referenceSignal : oneMinuteQuality;
 155:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 156:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 157:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 158:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(qualitySignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 159:         };
 160:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 161:         audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 162:         return result;
 163:     }

 165:     public ValidationResult validateSell(TradeSignal executionSignal) {
 166:         ValidationResult base = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, false);
 167:         if (!base.allowed()) return base;
 168: 
 169:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 170:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 171:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 172:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
 173:         }
 174:         if (!isBearish(fiveMinute.getDecision())) {
 175:             return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BEARISH",
 176:                     "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 5m decision was " + fiveMinute.getDecision() + ".");
 177:         }
 178: 
 179:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 180:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
 181:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
 182:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
 183:         }
 184:         if (isBullish(oneHour.getDecision())) {
 185:             return ValidationResult.reject("1H_BULLISH_VETO",
 186:                     "1m SELL was blocked because the fresh 1h strategic decision was " + oneHour.getDecision() + ".");
 187:         }
 188: 
 189:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "SELL_CONFIRMED",
 190:                 "1m SELL confirmed by fresh bearish 5m context; 1h is not bullish.");

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:350-470 =====
 350:     private ValidationResult validateBaseSignal(TradeSignal signal, boolean buy) {
 351:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 352:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution signal is missing required timing data.");
 353:         }
 354:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 355:             return ValidationResult.reject("NON_EXECUTION_TIMEFRAME",
 356:                     "Only 1m signals may trigger normal wallet execution. " + signal.getInterval() + " is context-only.");
 357:         }
 358:         if (buy && !isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
 359:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_BUY", "The 1m decision is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.");
 360:         }
 361:         if (!buy && !isBearish(signal.getDecision())) {
 362:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_SELL", "The 1m decision is not SELL/STRONG_SELL.");
 363:         }
 364:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BASE_VALID", "Base execution signal is valid.");
 365:     }
 366: 
 367:     private TradeSignal latestAtOrBefore(TradeSignal signal, String interval) {
 368:         return latestSignal(signal.getSymbol(), interval, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 369:     }
 370: 
 371:     private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> previousSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 372:         if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.previousBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
 373:         return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
 374:     }
 375: 
 376:     private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> latestSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 377:         if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.latestAtOrBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
 378:         return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
 379:     }
 380: 
 381:     private boolean isFresh(TradeSignal signal, Instant reference, Duration maximumAge) {
 382:         return signal != null
 383:                 && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null
 384:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)
 385:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));
 386:     }
 387: 
 388:     private boolean isBullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 389:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 390:     }
 391: 
 392:     private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 393:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 394:     }
 395: 
 396:     private boolean isWatchOrNeutral(SignalDecision decision) {
 397:         return decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
 398:     }
 399: 
 400:     private WalletSettings settings() {
 401:         return settingsRepository.findById(1L)
 402:                 .orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
 403:                         .id(1L)
 404:                         .executionProfile(ExecutionProfile.BALANCED.name())
 405:                         .requireNewBuyTransition(true)
 406:                         .dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true)
 407:                         .profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
 408:                         .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40))
 409:                         .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
 410:                         .build());
 411:     }
 412: 
 413:     private ValidationResult cap(ValidationResult result, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
 414:         if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
 415:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
 416:         if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();
 417:         return capped==result.positionPercent()?result:ValidationResult.allow(capped,result.code(),result.explanation()+" FIX-154 soft-HTF cap="+capped+"%.");
 418:     }
 419: 
 420:     /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
 421:     public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 422:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 423:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 424:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 425:         if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
 426:                 || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
 427:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
 428:         if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
 429:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softAddBlocked();
 430:             audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",
 431:                     "Existing raw 1h progressive-add guard blocked this add; FIX-154 audit only.");
 432:         }
 433:     }
 434: 
 435:     private void audit(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String path,
 436:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment, String finalResult, String reason) {
 437:         if (htfAuditStore == null) return;
 438:         try {
 439:             htfAuditStore.record(referenceSignal, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
 440:         } catch (RuntimeException auditFailure) {
 441:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.auditWriteFailed();
 442:             log.warn("FIX-154 audit handoff failed; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, signalId={}",
 443:                     path, referenceSignal == null ? null : referenceSignal.getId(), auditFailure);
 444:         }
 445:     }
 446: 
 447:     private void recordAssessmentMetric(HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
 448:         if (fix154Metrics != null && "1H_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code())
 449:                 && assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.NOT_TRUSTED) {
 450:             fix154Metrics.untrustedBearishOneHourBlocked();
 451:         }
 452:     }
 453: 
 454:     public record ValidationResult(boolean allowed, String code, String explanation, int positionPercent) {
 455:         public static ValidationResult allow(int positionPercent, String code, String explanation) {
 456:             return new ValidationResult(true, code, explanation, Math.max(1, Math.min(100, positionPercent)));
 457:         }
 458: 
 459:         public static ValidationResult reject(String code, String explanation) {
 460:             return new ValidationResult(false, code, explanation, 0);
 461:         }
 462:     }
 463: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java:1-200 =====
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;
   6: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   7: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   8: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
   9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  10: 
  11: import java.time.Duration;
  12: import java.time.Instant;
  13: import java.util.ArrayList;
  14: import java.util.List;
  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Builds execution evidence from a sequence of already-generated 1m BUY signals.
  18:  *
  19:  * Important architecture rule: this service NEVER creates a TradeSignal.  AnalysisService
  20:  * remains the single source of truth for signals.  Consolidation is an execution-only path
  21:  * that may approve the CURRENT fresh 1m BUY after several recent BUY signals have persisted.
  22:  */
  23: @Service
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: public class OpportunityConsolidationService {
  26: 
  27:     private static final String EXECUTION_INTERVAL = "1m";
  28:     private static final String CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL = "5m";
  29:     private static final String TREND_INTERVAL = "1h";
  30: 
  31:     private static final Duration OPPORTUNITY_WINDOW = Duration.ofMinutes(30);
  32:     private static final Duration FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(20);
  33:     private static final Duration ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofHours(3);
  34: 
  35:     private static final int MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS = 3;
  36:     private static final int STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS = 5;
  37:     private static final int MIN_AVERAGE_SCORE = 75;
  38:     private static final int MIN_AVERAGE_CONFIDENCE = 70;
  39: 
  40:     private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
  41:     private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
  42:     @Autowired(required = false)
  43:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  44:     @Autowired(required = false)
  45:     private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
  46: 
  47:     public Assessment evaluate(TradeSignal currentSignal) {
  48:         if (currentSignal == null || currentSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
  49:             return Assessment.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Current execution signal is missing.");
  50:         }
  51:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(currentSignal.getInterval()) || !isBullish(currentSignal.getDecision())) {
  52:             return Assessment.reject("NOT_1M_BUY", "Opportunity consolidation only evaluates fresh 1m BUY/STRONG_BUY signals.");
  53:         }
  54: 
  55:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  56:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  57: 
  58:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  59:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONTEXT", "No fresh 5m context is available for consolidation.");
  60:         }
  61:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  62:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", "No fresh 1h context is available for consolidation.");
  63:         }
  64:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment htf=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  65:         if(!htf.allowed()) {
  66:             audit(currentSignal, htf, htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  67:             return Assessment.reject(htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  68:         }
  69:         SignalDecision fiveDecision=htf.effectiveFiveMinute();
  70:         SignalDecision oneDecision=htf.effectiveOneHour();
  71: 
  72:         List<TradeSignal> recent = replayScope != null && replayScope.active()
  73:                 ? replayScope.recent(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), 20)
  74:                 : signalRepository.findTop20BySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
  75:         List<TradeSignal> consecutive = consecutiveFreshBuys(recent, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt());
  76: 
  77:         int count = consecutive.size();
  78:         if (count < MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS) {
  79:             Assessment result = Assessment.building(
  80:                     count,
  81:                     averageScore(consecutive),
  82:                     averageConfidence(consecutive),
  83:                     firstScore(consecutive),
  84:                     currentSignal.getTotalScore(),
  85:                     fiveDecision,
  86:                     oneDecision,
  87:                     "BUILDING",
  88:                     "Bullish opportunity is building: " + count + "/" + MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS
  89:                             + " consecutive fresh 1m BUY signals."
  90:             );
  91:             audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
  92:             return result;
  93:         }
  94: 
  95:         int averageScore = averageScore(consecutive);
  96:         int averageConfidence = averageConfidence(consecutive);
  97:         int firstScore = firstScore(consecutive);
  98:         int latestScore = currentSignal.getTotalScore();
  99:         boolean scoreStableOrImproving = latestScore >= firstScore - 3;
 100: 
 101:         if (averageScore < MIN_AVERAGE_SCORE || averageConfidence < MIN_AVERAGE_CONFIDENCE || !scoreStableOrImproving) {
 102:             Assessment result = Assessment.rejectDetailed(
 103:                     "QUALITY_DECAY",
 104:                     count,
 105:                     averageScore,
 106:                     averageConfidence,
 107:                     firstScore,
 108:                     latestScore,
 109:                     fiveDecision,
 110:                     oneDecision,
 111:                     "Persistent BUY signals exist, but quality is not stable enough. Average score=" + averageScore
 112:                             + ", average confidence=" + averageConfidence + ", first/latest score=" + firstScore + "/" + latestScore + "."
 113:             );
 114:             audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
 115:             return result;
 116:         }
 117: 
 118:         // Fully neutral 5m + 1h needs stronger persistence; this avoids treating repeated 1m noise as confirmation.
 119:         if (fiveDecision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 120:                 && oneDecision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 121:                 && count < STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS) {
 122:             Assessment result = Assessment.building(
 123:                     count,
 124:                     averageScore,
 125:                     averageConfidence,
 126:                     firstScore,
 127:                     latestScore,
 128:                     fiveDecision,
 129:                     oneDecision,
 130:                     "BUILDING_STRONGER_CONFIRMATION",
 131:                     "1m BUY persistence is valid, but both 5m and 1h are NEUTRAL. Waiting for "
 132:                             + STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS + " consecutive BUY signals or improving higher-timeframe context."
 133:             );
 134:             audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
 135:             return result;
 136:         }
 137: 
 138:         int positionPercent = consolidatedPositionPercent(count, fiveDecision, oneDecision);
 139:         if(htf.maxInitialPercent()!=null) positionPercent=Math.min(positionPercent,htf.maxInitialPercent());
 140:         String state = count >= STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS ? "CONFIRMED" : "BUILDING_CONFIDENCE";
 141:         String explanation = "Consolidated opportunity approved from the current signal #" + currentSignal.getId()
 142:                 + ": " + count + " consecutive fresh 1m BUY signals, average score=" + averageScore
 143:                 + ", average confidence=" + averageConfidence + ", score path=" + firstScore + "ƒ+'" + latestScore
 144:                 + ", 5m=" + fiveDecision + ", 1h=" + oneDecision
 145:                 + ". Reduced execution size=" + positionPercent + "%. No second trade signal was generated.";
 146: 
 147:         Assessment result = Assessment.allow(
 148:                 positionPercent,
 149:                 count,
 150:                 averageScore,
 151:                 averageConfidence,
 152:                 firstScore,
 153:                 latestScore,
 154:                 fiveDecision,
 155:                 oneDecision,
 156:                 state,
 157:                 explanation
 158:         );
 159:         audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
 160:         return result;
 161:     }
 162: 
 163:     private void audit(TradeSignal reference, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
 164:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
 165:         if (htfAuditStore == null) return;
 166:         try {
 167:             htfAuditStore.record(reference, "CONSOLIDATION", assessment, finalResult, reason);
 168:         } catch (RuntimeException ignored) {
 169:             // Best-effort evidence must never alter consolidation authority.
 170:         }
 171:     }
 172: 
 173:     private List<TradeSignal> consecutiveFreshBuys(List<TradeSignal> recent, Instant reference) {
 174:         List<TradeSignal> result = new ArrayList<>();
 175:         Instant cutoff = reference.minus(OPPORTUNITY_WINDOW);
 176:         for (TradeSignal signal : recent) {
 177:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)) {
 178:                 continue;
 179:             }
 180:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) {
 181:                 break;
 182:             }
 183:             if (!isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
 184:                 break;
 185:             }
 186:             result.add(signal);
 187:         }
 188:         return result;
 189:     }
 190: 
 191:     private int consolidatedPositionPercent(int count, SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour) {
 192:         int percent = count >= STRONG_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS ? 50 : 25;
 193:         if (fiveMinute == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 10;
 194:         if (isBullish(fiveMinute)) percent += 20;
 195:         if (oneHour == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 5;
 196:         if (isBullish(oneHour)) percent += 10;
 197:         return Math.min(75, percent);
 198:     }
 199: 
 200:     private int averageScore(List<TradeSignal> signals) {

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore.java:1-180 =====
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
   6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
   7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
   8: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
  10: 
  11: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  12: import java.time.Duration;
  13: import java.time.Instant;
  14: import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
  15: import java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException;
  16: 
  17: /**
  18:  * Best-effort, append-only FIX-154 evidence. It snapshots values then writes
  19:  * on a bounded dedicated executor: an audit insert never nests a database
  20:  * connection inside an execution transaction and never changes a BUY result.
  21:  */
  22: @Slf4j
  23: @Component
  24: public class BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore {
  25:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  26:     private final Executor executor;
  27:     private final Fix154Metrics metrics;
  28:     @Autowired(required = false)
  29:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  30: 
  31:     public BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore(JdbcTemplate jdbc, @Qualifier("fix154AuditExecutor") Executor executor,
  32:                                       Fix154Metrics metrics) {
  33:         this.jdbc = jdbc;
  34:         this.executor = executor;
  35:         this.metrics = metrics;
  36:     }
  37: 
  38:     public void record(TradeSignal reference, String path,
  39:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  40:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
  41:         // Replay signals are JDBC-generated and have no entity id. Replay parity
  42:         // uses its own evidence tables; never write a misleading Production audit.
  43:         if (reference == null || assessment == null || reference.getId() == null
  44:                 || (replayScope != null && replayScope.active())) return;
  45:         AuditPayload payload = AuditPayload.from(reference, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
  46:         try {
  47:             executor.execute(() -> persist(payload));
  48:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException rejected) {
  49:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  50:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit queue is full; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  51:                     path, reference.getId());
  52:         }
  53:     }
  54: 
  55:     private void persist(AuditPayload p) {
  56:         try {
  57:             jdbc.update("""
  58:                     INSERT INTO buy_htf_assessment_audit (
  59:                       reference_signal_id, evaluation_path, evaluated_at, replay_mode,
  60:                       one_hour_signal_id, one_hour_raw_decision, one_hour_effective_decision,
  61:                       one_hour_candle_close_time, one_hour_age_seconds, one_hour_data_quality_valid, one_hour_usability_reason,
  62:                       five_minute_signal_id, five_minute_raw_decision, five_minute_effective_decision,
  63:                       five_minute_candle_close_time, five_minute_age_seconds, five_minute_data_quality_valid, five_minute_usability_reason,
  64:                       confidence_threshold, assessment_band, hard_confirmation_present, max_initial_percent,
  65:                       legacy_veto_result, final_result, final_reason)
  66:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, FALSE, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  67:                     """,
  68:                     p.referenceId, p.path, Timestamp.from(p.evaluatedAt),
  69:                     p.one.id, p.one.decision, p.effectiveOne, timestamp(p.one.candleClose), p.one.ageSeconds, p.one.quality, p.one.usability,
  70:                     p.five.id, p.five.decision, p.effectiveFive, timestamp(p.five.candleClose), p.five.ageSeconds, p.five.quality, p.five.usability,
  71:                     HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD, p.band, p.hardConfirmationPresent, p.maxInitialPercent,
  72:                     p.legacyVeto, p.finalResult, p.reason);
  73:         } catch (Exception e) {
  74:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  75:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit was not recorded; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  76:                     p.path, p.referenceId, e);
  77:         }
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     private static Timestamp timestamp(Instant value) { return value == null ? null : Timestamp.from(value); }
  81: 
  82:     private record Context(Long id, String decision, Instant candleClose, Long ageSeconds, Boolean quality, String usability) {
  83:         static Context from(TradeSignal signal, Instant evaluatedAt) {
  84:             if (signal == null) return new Context(null, null, null, null, null, "MISSING");
  85:             Instant close = deriveCandleClose(signal);
  86:             Long age = close == null ? null : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(close, evaluatedAt).getSeconds());
  87:             return new Context(signal.getId(), signal.getDecision() == null ? null : signal.getDecision().name(), close, age,
  88:                     signal.getDataQualityValid(), Boolean.TRUE.equals(signal.getDataQualityValid()) ? "AVAILABLE" : "QUALITY_UNKNOWN");
  89:         }
  90:     }
  91:     private record AuditPayload(Long referenceId, String path, Instant evaluatedAt, Context one, Context five,
  92:                                 String effectiveOne, String effectiveFive, String band, boolean hardConfirmationPresent,
  93:                                 Integer maxInitialPercent, String legacyVeto, String finalResult, String reason) {
  94:         static AuditPayload from(TradeSignal reference, String path, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  95:                                  String finalResult, String reason) {
  96:             Instant evaluated = reference.getGeneratedAt() == null ? Instant.now() : reference.getGeneratedAt();
  97:             String legacy = "5M_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code()) || "1H_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code())
  98:                     ? assessment.code() : null;
  99:             return new AuditPayload(reference.getId(), path, evaluated,
 100:                     Context.from(assessment.rawOneHour(), evaluated), Context.from(assessment.rawFiveMinute(), evaluated),
 101:                     assessment.effectiveOneHour().name(), assessment.effectiveFiveMinute().name(), assessment.band().name(),
 102:                     assessment.hardConfirmationPresent(), assessment.maxInitialPercent(), legacy, finalResult, trim(reason));
 103:         }
 104:     }
 105:     private static Instant deriveCandleClose(TradeSignal signal) {
 106:         if (signal.getCandleOpenTime() == null || signal.getInterval() == null) return null;
 107:         long seconds = switch (signal.getInterval()) { case "1m" -> 60; case "5m" -> 300; case "1h" -> 3600; default -> 0; };
 108:         return seconds == 0 ? null : signal.getCandleOpenTime().plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1);
 109:     }
 110:     private static String trim(String value) { return value == null ? null : value.length() <= 1000 ? value : value.substring(0, 1000); }
 111: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/config/Fix154AuditAsyncConfig.java:1-80 =====
   1: package com.crypto.config;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
   4: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
   5: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
   6: import org.springframework.scheduling.concurrent.ThreadPoolTaskExecutor;
   7: 
   8: import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
   9: import java.util.concurrent.ThreadPoolExecutor;
  10: 
  11: /** Audit evidence must never borrow the connection held by an execution path. */
  12: @Configuration
  13: public class Fix154AuditAsyncConfig {
  14:     @Bean(name = "fix154AuditExecutor")
  15:     public Executor fix154AuditExecutor(@Value("${fix154.audit.queue-capacity:500}") int queueCapacity) {
  16:         var executor = new ThreadPoolTaskExecutor();
  17:         executor.setThreadNamePrefix("fix154-audit-");
  18:         executor.setCorePoolSize(1);
  19:         executor.setMaxPoolSize(1);
  20:         executor.setQueueCapacity(Math.max(1, queueCapacity));
  21:         executor.setRejectedExecutionHandler(new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
  22:         executor.initialize();
  23:         return executor;
  24:     }
  25: }

  22:                 "audit_write_failure_count", auditWriteFailures.get(),
  23:                 "soft_capped_entry_count", softCappedEntries.get(),

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/Fix154RuntimeSchemaChecks.java:1-100 =====
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.context.annotation.DependsOn;
   4: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: /**
   8:  * Runs during bean construction, before WebSocket admission and scheduled work.
   9:  * FIX-154 uses these columns in every mode, so this is intentionally not OFF-mode
  10:  * conditional and fails with a direct operational error rather than later SQL noise.
  11:  */
  12: @Component
  13: @DependsOn("entityManagerFactory")
  14: public class Fix154RuntimeSchemaChecks {

 415:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
 416:         if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();

 420:     /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
 421:     public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 422:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 423:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 424:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 425:         if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
 426:                 || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
 427:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
 428:         if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
 429:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softAddBlocked();
 430:             audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",
 431:                     "Existing raw 1h progressive-add guard blocked this add; FIX-154 audit only.");
 432:         }
 433:     }

 435:     private void audit(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String path,
 436:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment, String finalResult, String reason) {
 437:         if (htfAuditStore == null) return;
 438:         try {
 439:             htfAuditStore.record(referenceSignal, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
 440:         } catch (RuntimeException auditFailure) {
 441:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.auditWriteFailed();
 442:             log.warn("FIX-154 audit handoff failed; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, signalId={}",
 443:                     path, referenceSignal == null ? null : referenceSignal.getId(), auditFailure);
 444:         }
 445:     }

  16: /**
  17:  * Builds execution evidence from a sequence of already-generated 1m BUY signals.
  18:  *
  19:  * Important architecture rule: this service NEVER creates a TradeSignal.  AnalysisService
  20:  * remains the single source of truth for signals.  Consolidation is an execution-only path
  21:  * that may approve the CURRENT fresh 1m BUY after several recent BUY signals have persisted.
  22:  */

  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: public class OpportunityConsolidationService {

  34:

  47:     public Assessment evaluate(TradeSignal currentSignal) {
  48:         if (currentSignal == null || currentSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
  49:             return Assessment.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Current execution signal is missing.");
  50:         }
  51:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(currentSignal.getInterval()) || !isBullish(currentSignal.getDecision())) {
  52:             return Assessment.reject("NOT_1M_BUY", "Opportunity consolidation only evaluates fresh 1m BUY/STRONG_BUY signals.");
  53:         }

  55:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  56:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(currentSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);

  59:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONTEXT", "No fresh 5m context is available for consolidation.");
  60:         }
  61:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  62:             return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", "No fresh 1h context is available for consolidation.");
