  64:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment htf=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  65:         if(!htf.allowed()) {
  66:             audit(currentSignal, htf, htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  67:             return Assessment.reject(htf.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected consolidation.");
  68:         }
  69:         SignalDecision fiveDecision=htf.effectiveFiveMinute();
  70:         SignalDecision oneDecision=htf.effectiveOneHour();

  73:                 ? replayScope.recent(currentSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt(), 20)

  75:         List<TradeSignal> consecutive = consecutiveFreshBuys(recent, currentSignal.getGeneratedAt());
  76: 
  77:         int count = consecutive.size();
  78:         if (count < MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS) {
  79:             Assessment result = Assessment.building(
  80:                     count,
  81:                     averageScore(consecutive),

  83:                     firstScore(consecutive),

  85:                     fiveDecision,
  86:                     oneDecision,
  87:                     "BUILDING",
  88:                     "Bullish opportunity is building: " + count + "/" + MIN_CONSECUTIVE_BUYS
  89:                             + " consecutive fresh 1m BUY signals."
  90:             );
  91:             audit(currentSignal, htf, result.code(), result.explanation());
  92:             return result;
  93:         }
  94: 
  95:         int averageScore = averageScore(consecutive);
  96:         int averageConfidence = averageConfidence(consecutive);
  97:         int firstScore = firstScore(consecutive);
  98:         int latestScore = currentSignal.getTotalScore();
  99:         boolean scoreStableOrImproving = latestScore >= firstScore - 3;

 116:         }

  31:     // FIX-141: selection by candle close time, not generatedAt. generatedAt-only ordering let a
  32:     // backfilled/reprocessed signal for an OLD candle (stamped with a recent generatedAt) outrank
  33:     // a legitimately newer candle's signal whose generatedAt was earlier. candleOpenTime is the
  34:     // caller's look-ahead bound (evaluationTime minus the interval duration, i.e. the latest candle
  35:     // open time whose close has already happened); generatedAt still may not exceed evaluationTime
  36:     // either, so a signal can't be picked before it was actually generated.

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:90-145 =====
  90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }

 124:     private void context(String symbol, String interval, TradeSignal value) {
 125:         when(signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(
 126:                 symbol, interval, now)).thenReturn(Optional.of(value));
 127:     }
 128: 
 129:     private TradeSignal signal(Long id, String symbol, String interval, SignalDecision decision,
 130:                                Instant generatedAt, int score, int confidence) {
 131:         return TradeSignal.builder()
 132:                 .id(id)
 133:                 .symbol(symbol)
 134:                 .interval(interval)
 135:                 .decision(decision)
 136:                 .totalScore(score)
 137:                 .confidenceScore(confidence)
 138:                 .generatedAt(generatedAt)
 139:                 .build();
 140:     }

  17:     void replayNeverWritesAProductionAuditRow() {
  18:         JdbcTemplate jdbc = mock(JdbcTemplate.class);
  19:         BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore store = new BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore(jdbc, Runnable::run, new Fix154Metrics());
  20:         ExecutionReplayScope replay = new ExecutionReplayScope();
  21:         ReflectionTestUtils.setField(store, "replayScope", replay);
  22:         TradeSignal reference = signal(1L, "1m", SignalDecision.BUY, Instant.parse("2026-01-01T00:00:00Z"));
  23:         TradeSignal five = signal(null, "5m", SignalDecision.BUY, reference.getGeneratedAt().minusSeconds(60));
  24:         TradeSignal one = signal(null, "1h", SignalDecision.SELL, reference.getGeneratedAt().minusSeconds(3600));
  25:         one.setDataQualityValid(true);
  26:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = new HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService().assess(five, true, one, true);
  27:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replay.open(99L, List.of(reference, five, one), o -> { })) {
  28:             replay.reference(reference.getGeneratedAt());
  29:             store.record(reference, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, "BALANCED_STRONG", "test");
  30:         }
  31:         verifyNoInteractions(jdbc);
  32:     }
  33: 
  34:     private static TradeSignal signal(Long id, String interval, SignalDecision decision, Instant at) {
  35:         TradeSignal signal = new TradeSignal();
  36:         signal.setId(id); signal.setSymbol("BTCUSDT"); signal.setInterval(interval); signal.setDecision(decision);
  37:         signal.setGeneratedAt(at); signal.setCandleOpenTime(at.minusSeconds(switch (interval) { case "1m" -> 60; case "5m" -> 300; default -> 3600; }));
  38:         return signal;
  39:     }
  40: }

 245:     @Transactional
 246:     public synchronized Map<String, Object> archiveRun(long runId, String reason) {
 247:         Integer active = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=? AND status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')", Integer.class, runId);
 248:         if (active != null && active > 0) {
 249:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Test #" + runId + " is still running and cannot be archived.");
 250:         }

 252:         if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Regression test #" + runId + " not found.");
 253:         List<Map<String,Object>> existing = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id FROM regression_test_archive_batch WHERE source_test_run_id=?", runId);
 254:         if (!existing.isEmpty()) return Map.of("archiveBatchId", existing.get(0).get("id"), "sourceTestRunId", runId, "alreadyArchived", true);

 256:         KeyHolder kh = new GeneratedKeyHolder();
 257:         jdbcTemplate.update(c -> {
 258:             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement("INSERT INTO regression_test_archive_batch(source_test_run_id,test_name,symbol,start_time,end_time,source_status,archive_reason) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)", Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);

 737:      * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
 738:      * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
 739:      * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
 740:      */
 741:     private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
 742:         try {
 743:             java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 744:             java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 745:             columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
 746:             // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
 747:             // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
 748:             // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
 749:             columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
 750:             columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
 751:             columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);
 752: 
 753:             for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
 754:                 if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
 755:                 Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
 756:                 if (column == null) continue;
 757:                 field.setAccessible(true);
 758:                 Object value = field.get(signal);
 759:                 if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
 760:                 if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
 761:                 String columnName = column.name();
 762:                 if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
 763:                 columns.add(columnName);
 764:                 values.add(value);
 765:             }
 766: 
 767:             String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
 768:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
 769:         } catch (Exception e) {
 770:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
 771:         }
 772:     }
 773: 
 774:     private String snakeCase(String value) {
 775:         return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
 776:     }

 778:     private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
 779:         return switch (interval) {
 780:             case "1m" -> 60L;
 781:             case "5m" -> 300L;
 782:             case "1h" -> 3600L;
 783:             default -> 60L;
 784:         };
 785:     }

1043:                 && five.getTotalScore() >= 60 && five.getConfidenceScore() >= 65;

1964:     private ExecutionDecision accumulatedDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
1965:         if (!isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current)) {
1966:             return ExecutionDecision.observe("NO_BULLISH_EVIDENCE",
1967:                     "Current 1m signal does not add BUY/WATCH evidence.", e);
1968:         }
1969:         ImmediatePriceAction priceAction = immediatePriceAction(current);
1970:         if (!priceAction.supportive()) {
1971:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
1972:                     "IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_REVALIDATION_REQUIRED",
1973:                     "Accumulated evidence remains valid, but the latest fully closed 1m candle no longer confirms an "
1974:                             + "immediate entry (" + priceAction.summary() + "). The opportunity stays alive and must "
1975:                             + "wait for fresh price support; WEAK_UPTREND and ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE are not reclassified.",
1976:                     e);
1977:         }
1978:         if (e.fiveMinute() == null || e.oneHour() == null) {
1979:             return ExecutionDecision.building("MISSING_CONTEXT",
1980:                     "Opportunity is building, but fresh 5m/1h context is not yet available.", e);
1981:         }
1982:         if (isBearish(e.fiveMinute()) || isBearish(e.oneHour())) {
1983:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("HIGHER_TIMEFRAME_BEARISH",
1984:                     "Opportunity cancelled because 5m or 1h context is bearish.", e);
1985:         }
1986:         if (e.opportunityHealth() < 40) {
1987:             return ExecutionDecision.building("OPPORTUNITY_RECOVERING",
1988:                     "Bullish evidence is returning, but opportunity health is only "
1989:                             + e.opportunityHealth() + "/100 after recent bearish/aging penalties.", e);
1990:         }
1991:         if (e.evidenceScore() < MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE) {
1992:             return ExecutionDecision.building("EVIDENCE_BUILDING",
1993:                     "Bullish evidence is accumulating: score " + e.evidenceScore() + "/" + MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE
1994:                             + " from " + e.buyCount() + " BUY and " + e.watchCount() + " WATCH observations.", e);
1995:         }
1996:         if (e.buyCount() == 0 && e.watchCount() < 5) {
1997:             return ExecutionDecision.building("WATCH_ONLY_BUILDING",
1998:                     "WATCH evidence is persistent but has not yet produced a BUY signal; continuing to observe.", e);
1999:         }
2000: 
2001:         // WATCH-only accumulation is intentionally allowed to keep building across symbols,
2002:         // but old WATCH observations must not manufacture a fresh BUY when the current
2003:         // higher-timeframe context is only 1h WATCH + 5m NEUTRAL. In that state there is
2004:         // no fresh setup confirmation: keep the opportunity memory, but wait for either a
2005:         // 5m WATCH/BUY or a genuine 1h BUY before committing capital. Existing routes with
2006:         // real BUY evidence are unchanged, as are HTF_TRANSITION / IMMEDIATE_VALIDATION.
2007:         if (e.buyCount() == 0
2008:                 && !isBullish(e.oneHour())
2009:                 && e.fiveMinute() != SignalDecision.WATCH
2010:                 && !isBullish(e.fiveMinute())) {
2011:             return ExecutionDecision.building("WATCH_ONLY_NEEDS_FRESH_CONFIRMATION",
2012:                     "Accumulated WATCH evidence remains valid, but there is still no BUY observation and fresh "
2013:                             + "higher-timeframe confirmation is missing (5m=" + e.fiveMinute()
2014:                             + ", 1h=" + e.oneHour() + "). Wait for 5m WATCH/BUY or 1h BUY before entry.", e);
2015:         }
2016:         if (e.averageScore() < 65 || e.averageConfidence() < 65) {
2017:             return ExecutionDecision.building("EVIDENCE_QUALITY_LOW",
2018:                     "Evidence persists, but average quality is still low: score=" + e.averageScore()
2019:                             + ", confidence=" + e.averageConfidence() + ".", e);
2020:         }
2021: 
2022:         // FIX-091 / Fix 8: accumulated evidence is also forbidden from entering after the
2023:         // current setup has materially deteriorated from the recent opportunity peak. This
2024:         // directly protects the UNI/PEPE pattern where a strong BUY peak was followed by a
2025:         // weaker WATCH that later executed only because old evidence remained in memory.
2026:         EvidenceDeterioration deterioration = assessEvidenceDeterioration(current);
2027:         if (deterioration.material()) {
2028:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
2029:                     "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE_DETERIORATED",
2030:                     deterioration.explanation(),
2031:                     e);
2032:         }
2033: 
2034:         // FIX-021 / BICOUSDT + ETHUSDT:
2035:         // Accumulated evidence is memory, not a separate execution authority. It must obey
2036:         // the SAME configured 5m/1h profile that a normal direct BUY would face. This closes
2037:         // the contradiction where BALANCED rejected 5m WATCH + 1h NEUTRAL for a fresh BUY,
2038:         // but ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE later entered with that same or weaker HTF state.
2039:         TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult authority = validationService.validateBuyContext(current);
2040:         if (!authority.allowed()) {
2041:             return ExecutionDecision.building("ACCUMULATED_AUTHORITY_WAIT",
2042:                     "Accumulated evidence remains stored, but current HTF execution authority is insufficient: "
2043:                             + authority.code() + " - " + authority.explanation(), e);
2044:         }
2045: 
2046:         int percent = e.evidenceScore() >= STRONG_EVIDENCE_SCORE ? 50 : 25;
2047:         if (e.buyCount() >= 2) percent += 10;
2048:         if (e.fiveMinute() == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 5;
2049:         if (isBullish(e.fiveMinute())) percent += 15;
2050:         if (e.oneHour() == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 5;
2051:         if (isBullish(e.oneHour())) percent += 10;
2052:         // Never size accumulated evidence above the authority granted by the configured
2053:         // direct-BUY profile. Historical evidence may reduce uncertainty, not increase HTF authority.
2054:         percent = Math.min(Math.min(75, percent), authority.positionPercent());
2055:

2057:                 "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE",
2058:                 "OPPORTUNITY_CONFIRMED",

 115:     private Evaluation trendFollowing(
 116:             SignalDecision decision,
 117:             List<TradeSignal> contexts,
 118:             ContextCounts counts
 119:     ) {
 120:         if (isBullish(decision) && hasHardBearishOpposition(contexts)) {
 121:             return veto(ConfluenceStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT, SignalDecision.WATCH,
 122:                     "Trend-following long entry was vetoed by explicit strongly bearish higher-timeframe opposition.");
 123:         }
 124:         if (isBullish(decision) && hasExplicitBearishOpposition(contexts)) {
 125:             return preserve(ConfluenceStatus.CONFLICT, decision, true,
 126:                     "A bearish higher-timeframe decision reduced confidence, but did not veto the lower-timeframe BUY.");
 127:         }
 128:         if (isBullish(decision) && hasNeutralWeakStructure(contexts)) {
 129:             return preserve(ConfluenceStatus.CONFLICT, decision, true,
 130:                     "Higher-timeframe direction is NEUTRAL/WATCH with weak structure; confidence was reduced without blocking the BUY.");
 131:         }
 132:         if (isBearish(decision) && counts.strongBullish() > 0) {
 133:             return preserve(ConfluenceStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT, SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true,
 134:                     "Bearish signal was neutralized by strongly bullish higher-timeframe structure.");
 135:         }
 136:         if (isBearish(decision) && counts.bullish() > 0) {
 137:             return preserve(ConfluenceStatus.CONFLICT, SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, true,
 138:                     "Bearish signal conflicts with bullish higher-timeframe structure.");
 139:         }
 140:         return agreementOrMixed(decision, contexts, counts,
 141:                 "Trend-following setup is aligned with higher-timeframe direction.",
 142:                 "Higher-timeframe direction is mixed; no trend-following adjustment was applied.");
 143:     }
 144: 
 145: 
 146:     private boolean hasHardBearishOpposition(List<TradeSignal> contexts) {
 147:         return contexts.stream().anyMatch(signal ->
 148:                 signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL
 149:                         || (signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.SELL
 150:                         && signal.getTrendScore() <= STRONG_BEARISH_TREND_MAXIMUM));
 151:     }
 152: 
 153:     private boolean hasExplicitBearishOpposition(List<TradeSignal> contexts) {
 154:         return contexts.stream().anyMatch(signal -> signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.SELL);
 155:     }
 156: 
 157:     private boolean hasNeutralWeakStructure(List<TradeSignal> contexts) {
 158:         return contexts.stream().anyMatch(signal ->
 159:                 (signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 160:                         || signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH)
 161:                         && signal.getTrendScore() <= BEARISH_TREND_MAXIMUM);
 162:     }

===== codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:1-30 =====
   1: # Crypto AI Trader ƒ?" Architecture
   2: 
   3: Status labels used in this document:
   4: 
   5: - **Current** ƒ?" present in the repository today.
   6: - **Pending** ƒ?" approved design direction, not implemented or deployed.
   7: - **Legacy/shared** ƒ?" existing shared-market path retained as historical evidence
   8:   while the trader moves to local-candle ownership.
   9: 
  10: ## Current release status
  11: 
  12: **FIX-153 is implemented in source but is NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION.**
  13: It moves the trader toward collector-free, trader-owned local candle ingestion
  14: in `shared-market.mode=OFF`. Java 21, disposable-MySQL, crash/race, Windows
  15: two-process, soak, OFF-mode and Replay-parity proof gates remain open.
  16: 
  17: FIX-154 is a separate pending higher-timeframe design. It is not implemented
  18: and is not part of FIX-153 deployment scope.
  19: 
  20: ## System purpose
  21: 
  22: The application is a Spring Boot cryptocurrency trader that analyses Binance
  23: market data, persists candles and technical indicators, creates trade signals,
  24: and performs paper-wallet execution and position protection. Production and
  25: Replay are intended to use equivalent persisted market evidence.
  26: 
  27: ## Runtime and data ownership
  28:

===== codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:170-210 =====
 170: direct `PENDING` evidence; this must not block live trading.
 171: 
 172: The automatic resolver is valid only for the paper-trading phase, where every
 173: protection effect commits in the same transaction as its `APPLIED` update.
 174: Before deployment, the code must prove that there are no asynchronous,
 175: `REQUIRES_NEW`, in-memory-only, or external effects in this boundary. A future
 176: live-exchange order needs a separate idempotent/exchange-reconciliation design.
 177: 
 178: The target runtime also requires exactly one active trader process. This is an
 179: operational prerequisite, not a database lease or fence.
 180: 
 181: ## Pending: FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY validation
 182: 
 183: FIX-154 is separate from the local-candle change and is not implemented.
 184: 
 185: Its purpose is to let a historical bearish 1h signal affect a current 1m BUY
 186: only when the historical evidence is fresh, quality-valid, and sufficiently
 187: strong. `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` on a historical signal is investigatory
 188: evidence, not a higher-timeframe quality gate; the current 1m BTC protection
 189: remains unchanged.
 190: 
 191: The design includes reliability bands (`NOT_TRUSTED`, `SOFT_CONFLICT`,
 192: `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE`), required fresh/quality-valid bearish 5m support for
 193: a hard veto, persisted `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`, recovery release
 194: rules, immutable outcome audit evidence, and Replay/no-look-ahead parity.
 195: 
 196: The implementation design is maintained in `codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md`.
 197: It supersedes no existing deployed behavior and is deliberately separate from
 198: the unrelated legacy readiness document under `src/main/resources/md/FIX-146.md`.
 199: 
 200: ## Deployment posture
 201: 
 202: Neither FIX-153 nor FIX-154 is deployment-ready. FIX-153 source hardening is
 203: implemented but unverified; FIX-154 remains pending.
 204: 
 205: Required validation includes Java 21 build/tests, disposable MySQL bootstrap,
 206: real-MySQL concurrency and lock-wait tests, crash/restart tests, stale-input
 207: tests, Replay parity checks, OFF-mode health/admin checks, collector-free
 208: runtime verification, deterministic dispatcher/detector/drain tests,
 209: two-process Windows lock rehearsal, and sustained-load observation across
 210: enabled symbols and intervals. This local workspace has Java 17 and cannot

===== codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:245-285 =====
 245: of the WebSocket or protection path and is not a persisted trading fence.
 246: 
 247: ### Outcome-schema startup invariant
 248: 
 249: The active signal-processing runtime writes `candle_analysis_outcome` for
 250: terminal analysis evidence. V108 therefore ships with the FIX-154 release even
 251: though local-candle reconciliation remains disabled. `CandleAnalysisOutcomeSchemaVerifier`
 252: runs after Flyway and fails application startup with a clear message if the
 253: table is absent; this prevents later processing transactions from failing only
 254: when they attempt their first outcome write.
 255: # FIX-154 implementation update ƒ?" 2026-10-10
 256: 
 257: `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService` is the single pure assessment boundary for
 258: the 5m/1h BUY context. It returns effective decisions separately from the JPA
 259: entities, so a SOFT 1h conflict can be assessed as effective `NEUTRAL` without
 260: ever changing the persisted signal. `TradeExecutionValidationService` uses it
 261: for direct BUY and accumulated-evidence authority; `OpportunityConsolidationService`
 262: uses the same assessment for consolidation. A trusted SOFT bearish 1h limits an
 263: initial entry to 25%; a trusted HARD or unknown bearish 1h rejects it, while
 264: the legacy 5m bearish veto keeps priority.
 265: 
 266: `buy_htf_assessment_audit` is append-only operational evidence. Its writer is
 267: best-effort and uses a bounded dedicated executor: auditing never borrows an
 268: execution transaction's database connection, is skipped in Replay, and cannot
 269: be part of a BUY decision. The progressive add route retains its
 270: existing raw-1h guard; it writes `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` evidence only after
 271: that guard has already rejected an add.
 272: 
 273: `GET /api/fix154/health` exposes non-gating counters for untrusted bearish
 274: blocks, soft caps, soft add blocks, and best-effort audit failures.

 202:   immutable/transient and cannot mutate stored signals; progressive Soft audit
 203:   evidence is best-effort and cannot influence an add decision.
 204: - Design only: no Java, migration, or runtime behavior changed.
 205:

 209:   to reserve V107 for the pending FIX-154 migration. No applied migration was
 210:   edited.
 211: - Added `CandleAnalysisOutcomeSchemaVerifier`: startup now fails clearly if
 212:   `candle_analysis_outcome` is absent even though outcome-writing runtime code
 213:   is deployed. Added focused H2 coverage for the missing-table and present-table
 214:   paths; Java 21 focused tests pass.
 215: - V108 ships with FIX-154 because current live signal processing writes the
 216:   outcome table. Reconciliation remains disabled.

 218: # 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-154 implementation start: V107
 219: 
 220: - Confirmed read-only Flyway history: neither V107 nor V108 is applied to the
 221:   configured local trader database.
 222: - Added V107 as the first FIX-154 implementation artifact. It adds nullable
 223:   `trade_signal.data_quality_valid` and the append-only execution assessment
 224:   audit table. It deliberately has no `signal_mode` dependency or unique
 225:   execution-audit key.
 226: - No execution behavior has changed yet; BUY assessment/service integration is
 227:   pending implementation and review.
 228: 
 229: # 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-154 deployment-order and cap correction
 230: 
 231: - FIX-154 is now scheduled before remaining FIX-153 runtime work and no longer
 232:   depends on `signal_mode`. Its V107 migration is reserved from the actual
 233:   local Flyway history; the unreleased FIX-153 V107 must become V108 before
 234:   implementation.
 235: - Defined assessment-level `maxInitialPercent=25` for SOFT outcomes and required
 236:   every self-sizing caller to apply it. Kept progressive raw-1h support checks
 237:   unchanged; they already block add stages for bearish 1h evidence.
 238: - Corrected unknown-bearish/5m-veto priority and added SOFT counters.
 239: - Design only: no Java, migration, or runtime behavior changed.
 240: # 2026-10-10 ƒ?" FIX-154 trusted higher-timeframe BUY assessment (implementation)
 241: 
 242: - **Problem:** every bearish 1h row had equal veto authority, including
 243:   historical rows with unknown data-quality provenance; there was no durable
 244:   explanation of the effective 5m/1h BUY context.
 245: - **Root cause:** BUY validation and consolidation read raw `TradeSignal`
 246:   decisions directly, with no assessment of quality or bearish strength.
 247: - **Solution:** added `data_quality_valid` persistence to `TradeSignal` and
 248:   `AnalysisService`; introduced `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`; wired it
 249:   into direct BUY, accumulated-evidence authority, consolidation and
 250:   observational progressive-add evidence. The SOFT 1h result is capped at 25%
 251:   and uses immutable effective decisions; HARD and unknown bearish evidence
 252:   vetoes. `V107` creates `data_quality_valid` and append-only
 253:   `buy_htf_assessment_audit`.
 254: - **Files/classes:** `TradeSignal`, `AnalysisService`,
 255:   `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`, `TradeExecutionValidationService`,
 256:   `OpportunityConsolidationService`, `ExecutionIntelligenceService`,
 257:   `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore`, `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`,
 258:   and focused tests.
 259: - **Database impact:** new nullable `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`; no
 260:   backfill. Historical `NULL` stays unknown. New append-only audit table.
 261: - **Production / Replay impact:** the same shared validation service is used
 262:   for both. Audit failure is explicitly non-blocking. V108 ships because live
 263:   runtime outcome writers require its schema; reconciliation remains disabled.
 264: - **Validation status:** focused Maven tests could not run in this shell because
 265:   the active JDK reports `release version 21 not supported`; execute with the
 266:   project Java 21 toolchain before release.
 267: 
 268: ## Validation correction ƒ?" 2026-10-10
 269: 
 270: The shell was switched to the installed Java 21 toolchain
 271: (`C:\Program Files\Java\jdk-21`). Focused Maven verification then passed:
 272: `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
 273: `TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
 274: `OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.
 275: 
 276: ## FIX-154 review-round correction ƒ?" 2026-10-10
 277: 
 278: - **Replay parity:** V107 now brings `trade_signal_test` forward with the
 279:   V97 BTC freshness fields, V106 `signal_mode`, and FIX-154
 280:   `data_quality_valid`, matching the reflection-based Replay writer.
 281: - **Audit isolation:** audit records are snapshot and submitted to a bounded,
 282:   one-thread executor. Replay never writes a Production audit row, and audit
 283:   saturation/failure only increments an in-memory metric.
 284: - **Evidence accuracy:** audit candle close times are derived from candle open
 285:   time plus the interval, hard-confirmation is persisted, and both legacy veto
 286:   codes are retained.
 287: - **Startup safety:** outcome-schema verification now occurs at bean creation;
 288:   FIX-154 verifies its required production and replay columns before runtime
 289:   admission.
 290: - **Validation:** Java 21 focused tests passed, including the higher-timeframe
 291:   matrix, validation, consolidation and early outcome-schema verifier tests.
 292:

===== codex-docs/TRADING_RULES.md:1-60 =====
   1: # FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY rule ƒ?" 2026-10-10
   2: 
   3: - `data_quality_valid=true` is required before a bearish 1h signal can be
   4:   treated as trusted. `NULL` is unknown and fails closed for bearish evidence.
   5: - A trusted `STRONG_SELL` at `confidence_score >= 70` blocks the BUY.
   6: - A trusted `SELL`, or trusted `STRONG_SELL` below 70, is a SOFT conflict:
   7:   profiles receive effective 1h `NEUTRAL`, and any allowed initial BUY is
   8:   capped at 25%.
   9: - A bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any 1h result.
  10: - Existing progressive/add rules retain their raw 1h requirement; a SOFT 1h
  11:   never authorizes an add.

===== claude-docs/TRADING_RULES.md:115-135 =====
 115: 
 116: `PositionManagementService.analyze` is advisory except STOP_LOSS. A signal
 117: price is usable only if the candle closed at/after the position open
 118: (`PositionPriceAuthorityPolicy`). Order: TAKE_PROFIT -> profit lock ->
 119: STOP_LOSS -> PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT -> SELL/STRONG_SELL (suppressed while
 120: profit lock active; must pass `validateSell`: bearish 5m, no `1H_BULLISH_VETO`).
 121: Progressive add on 1m is possible; SL/TP only move up. Signal-path closes use
 122: `signal.latestPrice`; live closes use the tick price.
 123: 
 124: ## Pending (not implemented): FIX-154
 125: 
 126: Historical bearish 1h may block a current 1m BUY only when fresh, quality-valid
 127: and strong (`NOT_TRUSTED` / `SOFT_CONFLICT` / `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE`); hard
 128: veto needs fresh quality-valid bearish 5m. Legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` unchanged.

===== codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:1-30 =====
   1: # Crypto AI Trader ƒ?" Architecture
   2: 
   3: Status labels used in this document:
   4: 
   5: - **Current** ƒ?" present in the repository today.
   6: - **Pending** ƒ?" approved design direction, not implemented or deployed.
   7: - **Legacy/shared** ƒ?" existing shared-market path retained as historical evidence
   8:   while the trader moves to local-candle ownership.
   9: 
  10: ## Current release status
  11: 
  12: **FIX-153 is implemented in source but is NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION.**
  13: It moves the trader toward collector-free, trader-owned local candle ingestion
  14: in `shared-market.mode=OFF`. Java 21, disposable-MySQL, crash/race, Windows
  15: two-process, soak, OFF-mode and Replay-parity proof gates remain open.
  16: 
  17: FIX-154 is a separate pending higher-timeframe design. It is not implemented
  18: and is not part of FIX-153 deployment scope.
  19: 
  20: ## System purpose
  21: 
  22: The application is a Spring Boot cryptocurrency trader that analyses Binance
  23: market data, persists candles and technical indicators, creates trade signals,
  24: and performs paper-wallet execution and position protection. Production and
  25: Replay are intended to use equivalent persisted market evidence.
  26: 
  27: ## Runtime and data ownership
  28: 
  29: | Area | Current ownership | Target direction |
  30: |---|---|---|

 206: # 2026-10-09 ƒ?" V108 outcome-table startup guard
 207: 
 208: - Renumbered the unreleased FIX-153 reconciliation migration from V107 to V108
 209:   to reserve V107 for the pending FIX-154 migration. No applied migration was
 210:   edited.
 211: - Added `CandleAnalysisOutcomeSchemaVerifier`: startup now fails clearly if
 212:   `candle_analysis_outcome` is absent even though outcome-writing runtime code
 213:   is deployed. Added focused H2 coverage for the missing-table and present-table
 214:   paths; Java 21 focused tests pass.
 215: - V108 ships with FIX-154 because current live signal processing writes the
 216:   outcome table. Reconciliation remains disabled.
 217: 
 218: # 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-154 implementation start: V107
 219: 
 220: - Confirmed read-only Flyway history: neither V107 nor V108 is applied to the
 221:   configured local trader database.
 222: - Added V107 as the first FIX-154 implementation artifact. It adds nullable
 223:   `trade_signal.data_quality_valid` and the append-only execution assessment
 224:   audit table. It deliberately has no `signal_mode` dependency or unique
 225:   execution-audit key.
 226: - No execution behavior has changed yet; BUY assessment/service integration is
 227:   pending implementation and review.
 228: 
 229: # 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-154 deployment-order and cap correction
 230: 
 231: - FIX-154 is now scheduled before remaining FIX-153 runtime work and no longer
 232:   depends on `signal_mode`. Its V107 migration is reserved from the actual
 233:   local Flyway history; the unreleased FIX-153 V107 must become V108 before
 234:   implementation.
 235: - Defined assessment-level `maxInitialPercent=25` for SOFT outcomes and required
 236:   every self-sizing caller to apply it. Kept progressive raw-1h support checks
 237:   unchanged; they already block add stages for bearish 1h evidence.
 238: - Corrected unknown-bearish/5m-veto priority and added SOFT counters.
 239: - Design only: no Java, migration, or runtime behavior changed.
 240: # 2026-10-10 ƒ?" FIX-154 trusted higher-timeframe BUY assessment (implementation)
 241: 
 242: - **Problem:** every bearish 1h row had equal veto authority, including
 243:   historical rows with unknown data-quality provenance; there was no durable
 244:   explanation of the effective 5m/1h BUY context.
 245: - **Root cause:** BUY validation and consolidation read raw `TradeSignal`
 246:   decisions directly, with no assessment of quality or bearish strength.
 247: - **Solution:** added `data_quality_valid` persistence to `TradeSignal` and
 248:   `AnalysisService`; introduced `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`; wired it
 249:   into direct BUY, accumulated-evidence authority, consolidation and
 250:   observational progressive-add evidence. The SOFT 1h result is capped at 25%
 251:   and uses immutable effective decisions; HARD and unknown bearish evidence
 252:   vetoes. `V107` creates `data_quality_valid` and append-only
 253:   `buy_htf_assessment_audit`.
 254: - **Files/classes:** `TradeSignal`, `AnalysisService`,
 255:   `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`, `TradeExecutionValidationService`,
 256:   `OpportunityConsolidationService`, `ExecutionIntelligenceService`,
 257:   `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore`, `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`,
 258:   and focused tests.
 259: - **Database impact:** new nullable `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`; no
 260:   backfill. Historical `NULL` stays unknown. New append-only audit table.
 261: - **Production / Replay impact:** the same shared validation service is used
 262:   for both. Audit failure is explicitly non-blocking. V108 ships because live
 263:   runtime outcome writers require its schema; reconciliation remains disabled.
 264: - **Validation status:** focused Maven tests could not run in this shell because
 265:   the active JDK reports `release version 21 not supported`; execute with the
 266:   project Java 21 toolchain before release.
 267: 
 268: ## Validation correction ƒ?" 2026-10-10
 269: 
 270: The shell was switched to the installed Java 21 toolchain
 271: (`C:\Program Files\Java\jdk-21`). Focused Maven verification then passed:
 272: `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
 273: `TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
 274: `OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.
 275: 
 276: ## FIX-154 review-round correction ƒ?" 2026-10-10
 277: 
 278: - **Replay parity:** V107 now brings `trade_signal_test` forward with the
 279:   V97 BTC freshness fields, V106 `signal_mode`, and FIX-154
 280:   `data_quality_valid`, matching the reflection-based Replay writer.
 281: - **Audit isolation:** audit records are snapshot and submitted to a bounded,
 282:   one-thread executor. Replay never writes a Production audit row, and audit
 283:   saturation/failure only increments an in-memory metric.
 284: - **Evidence accuracy:** audit candle close times are derived from candle open
 285:   time plus the interval, hard-confirmation is persisted, and both legacy veto
 286:   codes are retained.
 287: - **Startup safety:** outcome-schema verification now occurs at bean creation;
 288:   FIX-154 verifies its required production and replay columns before runtime
 289:   admission.
 290: - **Validation:** Java 21 focused tests passed, including the higher-timeframe
 291:   matrix, validation, consolidation and early outcome-schema verifier tests.
 292: 
 293: ## FIX-154 closure work ƒ?" 2026-10-10

 296:   sizing, neutral-5m non-escalation, untrusted non-bearish context, quality
 297:   boundaries, and Replay audit suppression.
 298: - Extended the dedicated disposable MySQL migration test to assert V107 and

===== codex-docs/TRADING_RULES.md:1-60 =====
   1: # FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY rule ƒ?" 2026-10-10
   2: 
   3: - `data_quality_valid=true` is required before a bearish 1h signal can be
   4:   treated as trusted. `NULL` is unknown and fails closed for bearish evidence.
   5: - A trusted `STRONG_SELL` at `confidence_score >= 70` blocks the BUY.
   6: - A trusted `SELL`, or trusted `STRONG_SELL` below 70, is a SOFT conflict:
   7:   profiles receive effective 1h `NEUTRAL`, and any allowed initial BUY is
   8:   capped at 25%.
   9: - A bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any 1h result.
  10: - Existing progressive/add rules retain their raw 1h requirement; a SOFT 1h
  11:   never authorizes an add.

===== claude-docs/TRADING_RULES.md:115-135 =====
 115: 
 116: `PositionManagementService.analyze` is advisory except STOP_LOSS. A signal
 117: price is usable only if the candle closed at/after the position open
 118: (`PositionPriceAuthorityPolicy`). Order: TAKE_PROFIT -> profit lock ->
 119: STOP_LOSS -> PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT -> SELL/STRONG_SELL (suppressed while
 120: profit lock active; must pass `validateSell`: bearish 5m, no `1H_BULLISH_VETO`).
 121: Progressive add on 1m is possible; SL/TP only move up. Signal-path closes use
 122: `signal.latestPrice`; live closes use the tick price.
 123: 
 124: ## Pending (not implemented): FIX-154
 125: 
 126: Historical bearish 1h may block a current 1m BUY only when fresh, quality-valid
 127: and strong (`NOT_TRUSTED` / `SOFT_CONFLICT` / `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE`); hard
 128: veto needs fresh quality-valid bearish 5m. Legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` unchanged.

 124:     private void context(String symbol, String interval, TradeSignal value) {
 125:         when(signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(
 126:                 symbol, interval, now)).thenReturn(Optional.of(value));
 127:     }

 130:                                Instant generatedAt, int score, int confidence) {

 138:                 .generatedAt(generatedAt)
 139:                 .build();

===== src/main/resources/db/migration/V69__create_trade_signal_test_parity.sql:1-35 =====
   1: -- FIX-069: Replay keeps a full production-shaped TradeSignal snapshot for each generated signal.
   2: -- The table intentionally clones production trade_signal first, then adds only replay metadata.
   3: CREATE TABLE trade_signal_test LIKE trade_signal;
   4: -- Production enforces one signal per symbol/interval/candle. Replay must allow the same
   5: -- candle to be regenerated in multiple independent test runs, so run-scoping replaces
   6: -- the production-only uniqueness boundary.
   7: ALTER TABLE trade_signal_test DROP INDEX uk_trade_signal_symbol_interval_candle;
   8: ALTER TABLE trade_signal_test
   9:     ADD COLUMN test_run_id BIGINT NOT NULL AFTER id,
  10:     ADD COLUMN source_signal_id BIGINT NULL AFTER test_run_id,
  11:     ADD COLUMN replay_generated TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 AFTER source_signal_id,
  12:     ADD COLUMN generation_error VARCHAR(1000) NULL AFTER replay_generated,
  13:     ADD CONSTRAINT fk_trade_signal_test_run FOREIGN KEY (test_run_id) REFERENCES analysis_test_run(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  14:     ADD INDEX idx_trade_signal_test_run_time (test_run_id, generated_at),
  15:     ADD INDEX idx_trade_signal_test_run_interval (test_run_id, interval_code);
  16: 
  17: -- Safety archive remains backend-only even though the archive UI is removed.
  18: -- Composite key permits test-table IDs to restart from 1 after Clear Data.
  19: CREATE TABLE trade_signal_test_archive LIKE trade_signal_test;
  20: ALTER TABLE trade_signal_test_archive
  21:     DROP PRIMARY KEY,
  22:     MODIFY COLUMN id BIGINT NOT NULL,
  23:     ADD COLUMN archive_batch_id BIGINT NOT NULL FIRST,
  24:     ADD PRIMARY KEY (archive_batch_id, id),
  25:     ADD INDEX idx_trade_signal_test_archive_run_time (archive_batch_id, test_run_id, generated_at);

===== codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md:30-180 =====
  30: 
  31: ## Immutable signal evidence
  32: 
  33: Migration `V107` adds:
  34: 
  35: ```sql
  36: ALTER TABLE trade_signal ADD COLUMN data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL;
  37: ```
  38: 
  39: `AnalysisService` writes `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` through the
  40: existing `TradeSignal.builder()` call for every newly generated signal on every
  41: interval. Historic rows are never backfilled: `NULL` means unknown/invalid.
  42: 
  43: FIX-154 does not depend on `signal_mode` or any other FIX-153 runtime/schema
  44: feature. `data_quality_valid = true` is sufficient evidence eligibility for
  45: this release; false or `NULL` is unusable. Historical-only exclusion is added
  46: later with FIX-153, after its own migration/runtime release is approved.
  47: 
  48: Historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` is audit evidence only. It is never a
  49: FIX-154 usability or hard-veto predicate. The current 1m BTC guard is unchanged.
  50: 
  51: ## Time-bounded selection and fixed freshness
  52: 
  53: For a request with reference signal `R`, use `R.generatedAt` as `evaluatedAt`.
  54: Select the latest same-symbol 5m and 1h signal such that both its generated
  55: time and its candle close are at or before `evaluatedAt`; no wall-clock lookup
  56: is permitted. Replay supplies the same historical reference time.
  57: 
  58: Existing fixed freshness limits remain authoritative:
  59: 
  60: | Interval | Maximum age |
  61: |---|---:|
  62: | 5m | 20 minutes |
  63: | 1h | 3 hours |
  64: 
  65: `MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` and `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` retain their existing
  66: meaning and priority when their respective interval is missing or stale. A
  67: stale signal is not quietly converted into an allowed BUY just because it is
  68: `NOT_TRUSTED` for FIX-154.
  69: 
  70: ## Usability and 1h strength
  71: 
  72: A fresh row is usable for FIX-154 only if `data_quality_valid = true`.
  73: 
  74: FIX-154 v1 does not invent ADX/DMI/VWAP/CMF evidence, because those indicators
  75: are not currently persisted. Its fixed strength definition is:
  76: 
  77: ```text
  78: HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE = 1h decision == STRONG_SELL
  79:                          AND confidence_score >= 70
  80: SOFT_CONFLICT          = fresh, quality-valid 1h decision == SELL
  81:                          OR STRONG_SELL with confidence_score below 70
  82: NOT_TRUSTED            = fresh but quality-invalid/unknown/historical-only 1h
  83:                          OR fresh non-bearish 1h
  84: ```
  85: 
  86: `confidence_score` is the persisted effective confidence. `raw_confidence_score`
  87: is explicitly not used. The constant `70` is a named FIX-154 configuration
  88: default, persisted in the audit row, and may change only through a reviewed
  89: strategy change with Replay regression.
  90: 
  91: ## Exact 1h A- 5m decision matrix
  92: 
  93: First apply existing missing/stale checks. Only then apply this table.
  94: 
  95: | 1h state | 5m state | Final HTF result before profile | Profile inputs |
  96: |---|---|---|---|
  97: | missing/stale | any | existing `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` rejection | none |
  98: | any | missing/stale | existing `MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` rejection | none |
  99: | NOT_TRUSTED bearish from false/NULL quality | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
 100: | NOT_TRUSTED bearish from false/NULL quality | bullish/neutral/watch | `1H_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
 101: | NOT_TRUSTED non-bearish from false/NULL quality | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
 102: | NOT_TRUSTED non-bearish from false/NULL quality | bullish/neutral/watch | continue to profile | raw 1h, raw 5m |
 103: | SOFT_CONFLICT | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
 104: | SOFT_CONFLICT | bullish/neutral/watch | continue to profile, cap resulting first entry at 25% | effective 1h=`NEUTRAL`, raw 5m |
 105: | HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE | bearish, quality-valid | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO`; audit `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT` | none |
 106: | HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE | bullish/neutral/watch, quality-valid | `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
 107: | fresh, quality-valid non-bearish 1h | non-bearish | continue to profile | raw 1h, raw 5m |
 108: 
 109: The profile call produces the final decision for every `continue to profile`
 110: row. It may still return its existing insufficient-confirmation code. It must
 111: not consult the raw 1h row after this mapping.
 112: 
 113: `5m quality=false/NULL` does not alter the legacy 5m fallback. A fresh raw
 114: bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO`, exactly as today. It merely cannot
 115: act as new FIX-154 hard-veto confirmation or a soft-conflict release.
 116: 
 117: The veto order is fixed: a bearish 5m returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any
 118: trusted-1h result. If the 1h is hard, audit records `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT`
 119: on that legacy outcome. When 5m is not bearish, a hard trusted 1h returns
 120: `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`. The only deliberate lasting relaxation is a trusted
 121: SOFT bearish 1h: an otherwise allowed initial entry is capped at 25%.
 122: 
 123: ## Profile contract
 124: 
 125: `conservativeBuy`, `balancedBuy`, `balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException`, and
 126: `aggressiveBuy` receive the assessment's effective 1h/5m contextƒ?"not raw
 127: selected 1h evidence. A SOFT conflict maps to `NEUTRAL`, never `WATCH`, then
 128: the validation result is capped with `min(profilePositionPercent, 25)`.
 129: This means the neutral-5m/direct-BUY exception still requires a genuinely
 130: bullish 1h and cannot be unlocked by SOFT evidence. An untrusted non-bearish
 131: 1h retains its raw decision so deployment does not accidentally tighten current
 132: bullish authority.
 133: 
 134: The assessment result carries `maxInitialPercent`: `25` for `SOFT_CONFLICT`,
 135: otherwise no cap. Every caller that selects a size must use
 136: `min(itsCalculatedPercent, maxInitialPercent)` when the cap is present. This
 137: includes direct validation, `validateBuyContext()` callers (the 5m-trigger
 138: path and accumulated evidence), and
 139: `OpportunityConsolidationService.consolidatedPositionPercent()`.
 140: 
 141: The raw selected signals remain audit evidence. Existing 5m requirements inside
 142: profiles remain unchanged. HARD and untrusted-bearish outcomes never call a
 143: profile method.
 144: 
 145: ### Add-on contract
 146: 
 147: `ExecutionIntelligenceService.progressivePositionDecision()` already reads raw
 148: 1h evidence and requires `WATCH` or bullish support for `SCOUT_ENTRY`,
 149: `CONFIRMATION_ADD`, and `PROGRESSIVE_TREND_ADD`. Keep that check unchanged and
 150: outside the three replacement paths; it already blocks HARD, SOFT, and
 151: untrusted-bearish 1h states. Do not route it through effective FIX-154 context,
 152: which could widen behavior. When that existing check rejects while the assessed
 153: band is SOFT, record `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` as audit/metric evidence only.
 154: 
 155: ## Append-only execution audit
 156: 
 157: FIX-154 is evaluated during execution/consolidation, not while a signal is
 158: saved. Migration `V107` creates append-only `buy_htf_assessment_audit`:
 159: 
 160: - primary key ID;
 161: - reference signal ID, evaluation path (`DIRECT_BUY`, `BUY_CONTEXT`,
 162:   `CONSOLIDATION`, `PROGRESSIVE_TREND_ADD`, `CONFIRMATION_ADD`,
 163:   `ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE_ADD`) and `evaluated_at`; no unique constraint;
 164: - raw/effective 1h and 5m signal IDs, decisions, signal modes, candle closes,
 165:   ages, data-quality values, and usability reasons;
 166: - configured confidence threshold, band, legacy-veto result, profile context,
 167:   final result/reason, Production/Replay mode, and creation timestamp.
 168: 
 169: The audit is written best-effort after the relevant execution/consolidation
 170: decision is known. A duplicate or persistence failure is logged and increments
 171: `fix154.audit_write_failure_count`; it must never roll back, reject, or alter a
 172: trading decision. It is evidence onlyƒ?"not a fence or an input to later trading.
 173: 
 174: ## Deployment behavior
 175: 
 176: Immediately after deployment all pre-V107 1h/5m rows have
 177: `data_quality_valid=NULL`. They are treated as `NOT_TRUSTED`; they cannot make a
 178: new permissive decision: a bearish unknown 1h remains blocked, while a
 179: non-bearish unknown 1h retains its existing profile authority. The health view
 180: must expose `fix154.untrusted_bearish_1h_block_count` and

===== codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md:210-280 =====
 210: - Historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` does not change usability; current
 211:   1m BTC behavior is unchanged.
 212: - FIX-154 migration succeeds with no `signal_mode` column and its assessment
 213:   has no FIX-153 schema/runtime dependency.
 214: - No future signal/candle is selected in Production or Replay; both produce
 215:   equivalent audit evidence for the same historical inputs.
 216: - Pre-migration `NULL` quality is never treated as valid.
 217: - Repeated execution/consolidation evaluations append audit rows and audit
 218:   failure leaves the business decision intact.
 219: - A SOFT initial entry never exceeds 25% through progressive, confirmation, or
 220:   accumulated-evidence or consolidation sizing; existing progressive raw-1h
 221:   support rules block add stages while the 1h remains bearish.
 222: 
 223: ## Release conditions
 224: 
 225: Run Java 21 unit/integration tests, migration tests, Production/Replay parity,
 226: and a bounded no-look-ahead historical replay before enabling FIX-154.
 227: FIX-153 remains independent and retains its own release gates.
 228: 
 229: ## Revision addendum ƒ?" Round 5 approval conditions
 230: 
 231: This addendum governs where it differs from earlier wording.
 232: 
 233: ### Release build decision
 234: 
 235: The FIX-154 release build includes `V108__fix_153_local_candle_reconciliation.sql`
 236: after the current unreleased FIX-153 V107 migration is renumbered to V108.
 237: This is required even with reconciliation disabled: `candle_analysis_outcome`
 238: is used by ordinary live signal processing, terminal worker outcomes, and the
 239: closed-candle detector. Omitting V108 would cause missing-table SQL failures;
 240: in particular, a normal signal-processing completion would roll back because
 241: its outcome write is inside the processing transaction.
 242: 
 243: Including V108 ships FIX-153 reconciliation *schema only*. The reconciliation
 244: runtime remains disabled and retains all of its separate release gates.
 245: 
 246: ### No FIX-153 provenance dependency
 247: 
 248: Ignore earlier references to historical-only status or signal modes in the
 249: FIX-154 band definition and audit payload. FIX-154 v1 eligibility depends only
 250: on time-bounded selection and `data_quality_valid = true`; `signal_mode` is not
 251: read, required, or stored by the FIX-154 audit.
 252: 
 253: ### Effective context is transient
 254: 
 255: The assessment returns immutable effective decision values separately from the
 256: loaded `TradeSignal` entities. No code may call `setDecision`, mutate, merge,
 257: or save a selected 1h/5m signal to obtain effective `NEUTRAL` context. Profile
 258: methods receive explicit effective decisions (or an immutable assessment
 259: context), while the raw entity remains audit evidence only. A regression test
 260: must load a SOFT 1h signal, execute the assessment/profile path, flush/clear,
 261: and prove its stored decision is unchanged.
 262: 
 263: ### Progressive-path evidence is observational
 264: 
 265: `progressivePositionDecision()` retains its existing raw-1h support check as
 266: the only decision authority for scout/add paths. If that existing check passes,
 267: FIX-154 performs no assessment lookup and writes no progressive audit row. If
 268: it rejects because raw 1h is bearish, a best-effort audit-only assessment may
 269: record `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` when the band is SOFT. That observation must not
 270: alter the rejection, allow an add, cause a database lookup on passing paths, or
 271: propagate an audit failure into the trading decision.
 272: 
 273: V108 ships with FIX-154 because the current runtime writes
 274: `candle_analysis_outcome`. Reconciliation remains disabled.
 275: 
 276: ### Round 5 text clarification
 277: 
 278: This addendum removes any earlier contrary implication: FIX-154 v1 has no
 279: `signal_mode` or historical-only predicate or audit field. Effective 1h/5m
 280: decisions are separate immutable values and never mutate a loaded signal.

 202: ## FIX-154: pending higher-timeframe BUY validation
 203: 
 204: FIX-154 is a separate, not-yet-implemented design. Do not merge it into the
 205: local-candle change or describe it as deployed.
 206: 
 207: Purpose: a historical bearish 1h signal may block or delay a current 1m BUY
 208: only when its own evidence is fresh, data-quality-valid, and sufficiently
 209: strong. A historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` must remain investigatory
 210: evidence, not a higher-timeframe validity gate; the current 1m signal's own
 211: BTC protection remains unchanged.
 212: 
 213: - Score usable bearish 1h evidence into `NOT_TRUSTED`, `SOFT_CONFLICT`, or
 214:   `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE` using closed-candle local indicators.
 215: - A hard veto requires strong 1h evidence plus fresh, quality-valid bearish
 216:   5m confirmation. Stale, invalid, or unknown historical quality can never
 217:   hard-veto.
 218: - Persist `trade_signal.data_quality_valid` from
 219:   `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` for every new signal. Historical NULL
 220:   means unknown/invalid; never backfill it to true.
 221: - A confirmed 1h recovery may release only a soft conflict and only with fresh,
 222:   quality-valid, non-bearish 5m evidence.
 223: - Preserve the legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` behavior: the new data-quality gate
 224:   applies to the new hard-veto/recovery paths, not that legacy fallback.
 225: - Add immutable BUY-evaluation audit evidence; maintain Replay parity and

 218:     private ValidationResult balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 219:             TradeSignal executionSignal, SignalDecision fiveMinute, SignalDecision oneHour, int entryQualityScore) {
 220:         ValidationResult balanced = balancedBuy(fiveMinute, oneHour);
 221:         if (balanced.allowed()) return balanced;
 222: 
 223:         boolean neutralFiveBullishOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && isBullish(oneHour);
 224:         BtcContextStatus btcStatus = executionSignal.getBtcContextStatus();
 225:         // FIX-112B: plain/strong BTC conflict cannot be rescued by this exception.
 226:         // FIX-141: nor can a failed freshness guard - STALE_CONTEXT is a real, evidenced
 227:         // block (missing/stale/future-dated/unknown-candle-time BTC context), not an
 228:         // "unavailable, non-authoritative" state. This exception must not restore the
 229:         // entry permission the freshness guard already withheld upstream.
 230:         // Other BTC states retain their existing upstream Production semantics.
 231:         boolean btcSafeForException = btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT
 232:                 && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STRONG_CONFLICT
 233:                 && btcStatus != BtcContextStatus.STALE_CONTEXT;

 235:                 && entryQualityScore >= 70
 236:                 && btcSafeForException;

 239:             return ValidationResult.allow(25, "BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M",

 241:                              + "rather than bearish while 1h remains " + oneHour

 245:                             + ", entryQuality=" + entryQualityScore
 246:                             + ", btcStatus=" + btcStatus
 247:                             + ", position=25%.");

 249: 
 250:         // FIX-11G / controlled Production behavior change:
 251:         // An already-approved fresh 1m BUY can appear before tactical 5m confirmation
 252:         // catches up. When 5m is still NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH, neither timeframe is
 253:         // bearish, but the original BALANCED matrix has no authority for this transition.
 254:         // Grant only a 25% exploratory authority and only when FinalDecision already
 255:         // allowed entry, BTC has no hard conflict, and Entry Quality is at/above the
 256:         // existing chase cutoff. Do NOT add a second confidence threshold here: the
 257:         // upstream BUY + finalEntryAllowed decision already owns signal confidence.
 258:         //
 259:         // Golden rule: Replay = Production. Replay reaches this exact shared method
 260:         // through ExecutionReplayScope historical 5m/1h lookups; there is no Replay-only
 261:         // copy, threshold, or special execution behavior. validateBuyContext() remains
 262:         // intentionally unchanged so accumulated-evidence/recovery authority is not widened.
 263:         boolean neutralFiveWatchOne = fiveMinute == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL && oneHour == SignalDecision.WATCH;
 264:         if (neutralFiveWatchOne) {
 265:             boolean upstreamApproved = executionSignal.isFinalEntryAllowed();
 266:             boolean entryQualitySafe = entryQualityScore >= BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY;
 267:             // FIX-141: see btcSafeForException above - STALE_CONTEXT must not be rescued here either.

 272:             if (upstreamApproved && entryQualitySafe && btcSafeForTransitionalAuthority) {

 276:                         executionSignal.getDecision(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(),

 280:                                 + "1m BUY while 5m is NEUTRAL and 1h is WATCH. "

 300:     private ValidationResult balancedBuy(SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one) {
 301: 
 302:         if (isBullish(five) && isBullish(one)) {
 303:             return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BALANCED_FULL",
 304:                     "Balanced profile approved 100%: both 5m and 1h are bullish.");
 305:         }
 306:         if (isBullish(five) && isWatchOrNeutral(one)) {
 307:             return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
 308:                     "Balanced profile approved 75%: 5m is bullish while 1h is " + one + ".");
 309:         }
 310:         if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && isBullish(one)) {
 311:             return ValidationResult.allow(75, "BALANCED_STRONG",
 312:                     "Balanced profile approved 75%: 1h is bullish while 5m is WATCH.");
 313:         }
 314:         if (five == SignalDecision.WATCH && one == SignalDecision.WATCH) {
 315:             return ValidationResult.allow(50, "BALANCED_EARLY",
 316:                     "Balanced profile approved 50%: both 5m and 1h are WATCH, so this is an early reduced entry.");
 317:         }
 318: 
 319:         return ValidationResult.reject("BALANCED_CONFIRMATION_INSUFFICIENT",
 320:                 "Balanced profile requires bullish/WATCH confirmation. Current 5m=" + five + ", 1h=" + one + ".");
 321:     }
 322: 
 323:     private ValidationResult aggressiveBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one) {
 324:         ValidationResult balanced = balancedBuy(five, one);
 325:         if (balanced.allowed()) return balanced;
 326: 
 327:         boolean probeAlignment = five == SignalDecision.WATCH && one == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
 328:         if (!probeAlignment) {
 329:             return ValidationResult.reject("AGGRESSIVE_CONFIRMATION_INSUFFICIENT",
 330:                     "Aggressive profile still requires 5m WATCH or better and a non-bearish 1h context. Current 5m="
 331:                             + five + ", 1h=" + one + ".");
 332:         }
 333: 
 334:         int confidence = executionSignal.getConfidenceScore();
 335:         int trend = executionSignal.getTrendScore();
 336:         int volume = executionSignal.getVolumeScore();
 337:         int momentum = executionSignal.getMomentumScore();
 338: 
 339:         if (confidence < 85 || trend < 22 || volume < 16 || momentum < 12) {
 340:             return ValidationResult.reject("AGGRESSIVE_PROBE_QUALITY",
 341:                     "Aggressive 25% probe requires confidence>=85, trend>=22, volume>=16, momentum>=12. "
 342:                             + "Actual confidence=" + confidence + ", trend=" + trend + ", volume=" + volume
 343:                             + ", momentum=" + momentum + ".");
 344:         }
 345: 
 346:         return ValidationResult.allow(25, "AGGRESSIVE_PROBE",
 347:                 "Aggressive profile approved a 25% probe: 1m BUY is high quality, 5m is WATCH, and 1h is NEUTRAL.");
 348:     }

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** FIX-154 is materially implemented in the current working tree, not merely documented: V107 adds nullable `trade_signal.data_quality_valid` and `buy_htf_assessment_audit`; `AnalysisService` persists `marketContext.dataValid()`; and the shared assessment is used by direct BUY, BUY-context, and consolidation. [V107](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql:3) [AnalysisService](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/AnalysisService.java:406) [validation](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:81) [consolidation](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java:55)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** New signals persist a primitive `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` into nullable `TradeSignal.dataQualityValid`; V107 adds the column without an update/backfill, so historic rows remain `NULL` and are treated as untrusted. [snapshot](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/dto/MarketContextSnapshot.java:18) [entity](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/TradeSignal.java:71) [assessment](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java:46)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The core 1h policy exists: trusted SOFT 1h maps to detached effective `NEUTRAL` and a 25% cap; trusted HARD 1h vetoes; bearish 5m retains priority. The selected entities are not mutated by this service. [assessment](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java:20) [sizing cap](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:413)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `btcContextEntryAllowed` is not used by the FIX-154 assessment, direct validation, consolidation, or audit store. Thus it is not a new FIX-154 trust/hard-veto predicate. It remains an execution gate on other legacy routes, including a 5m setup trigger and HTF-transition route. [assessment](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java:15) [5m trigger](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:355) [HTF transition](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:1427)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Selection/freshness diverges from the design. FIX-154 selects and ages solely by `generatedAt`; it does not bound `candleOpenTime`/candle close. The repository and Replay scope already contain close-bounded APIs but these call sites do not use them. [current lookup](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:367) [freshness](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:381) [available repository API](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/TradeSignalRepository.java:31) [design requirement](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md:51)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** 5m quality is not evaluated by the assessment. A false/`NULL` bearish 5m still sets `hardConfirmationPresent=true` if the 1h is hard, and a false/`NULL` non-bearish 5m can release a SOFT conflict. This conflicts with the design’s rule that invalid 5m data cannot provide new hard-confirmation or SOFT-release evidence. [code](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java:22) [design](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md:105)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Production audit persistence is append-only and best-effort, but Replay returns before writing and the SQL hard-codes `replay_mode=FALSE`. This does not provide the required equivalent Production/Replay audit evidence. [audit store](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore.java:38) [migration](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql:18)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Audit schema/payload is partial versus the design: it has no profile-context column; it merges false and `NULL` usability into `QUALITY_UNKNOWN`; and actual path strings differ (`ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE`, `SETUP_WAKEUP`, `PROGRESSIVE_ADD`) from the contracted registry. [audit payload](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore.java:82) [paths](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:123) [setup caller](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:434) [design registry](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md:160)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** A health endpoint exists—correcting the prior finding—but its four counters are aggregate, not grouped by execution path. Consolidation has no metrics dependency, so it cannot increment the untrusted-block or soft-cap metrics; Replay can increment validation metrics. [endpoint](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/Fix154DiagnosticsController.java:9) [metrics](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/Fix154Metrics.java:10) [consolidation](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java:40)

The only FIX-154 property is the audit queue capacity, defaulting to 500; there is no enable switch. ## OBJECTIONS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No `agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md` exists, despite AGENTS.md requiring it before material implementation.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The 5m-quality, candle-close selection, Replay-audit, `signal_mode`, progressive-observation, metric-path, and consolidation-code gaps are material divergences from the named design.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The changelog’s 70-threshold support is a count of rows, not outcome evidence. [changelog](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/CHANGELOG_AI.md:192)

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** There is no demonstrated out-of-sample, fee/slippage-aware, or Production/Replay parity evidence that the 25% SOFT relaxation improves trading performance. No such performance conclusion should be drawn from this implementation review.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** I did not run tests, migrations, Replay, or database queries; this is a static, read-only working-tree review.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Compile status, migration success on a real MySQL schema, archive failure reproduction, and Production state remain unverified.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Existing non-FIX-154 raw HTF/BTC gates may predate this work; the current code proves they remain, but not when or why they were introduced.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No external research was used; all substantive conclusions above are from the repository.
